"""Validation interventions affecting compression geometry and context retrieval."""
import argparse
from contextlib import contextmanager
import json
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift
from train_CASA import make_loader, mask_boundary


@contextmanager
def intervention(context, mode):
    point = context.geometric_point
    distance = context.squared_distance
    gate = context.gate.forward
    gamma = context.gamma_context.detach().clone()
    if mode == 'context_off':
        with torch.no_grad():
            context.gamma_context.zero_()
    elif mode == 'fixed_radius':
        def equalize_radius(projected):
            space = point(projected)[..., 1:]
            norm = space.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
            radial = torch.asinh(norm)
            positions = tuple(range(2, radial.ndim - 1))
            nonzero = (norm > 0).sum(positions, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1)
            mean_radius = radial.sum(positions, keepdim=True) / nonzero
            # Origins retain their zero vector; the full-position mean is preserved.
            return lift(F.normalize(space, dim=-1) * mean_radius.sinh())
        context.geometric_point = equalize_radius
    elif mode == 'fixed_direction':
        def radial_only(projected):
            direction = torch.zeros_like(projected)
            direction[..., 0] = projected.norm(dim=-1)
            return point(direction)
        context.geometric_point = radial_only
    elif mode == 'gate_open':
        context.gate.forward = lambda x: x.new_full((x.shape[0], context.heads, *x.shape[-2:]), 20.)
    elif mode == 'euclidean_metric':
        def euclidean(query, key):
            query, key = query[..., 1:], key[..., 1:]
            return (query.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True) + key.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                    - 2 * (query @ key.transpose(-1, -2))).clamp_min(0)
        context.squared_distance = euclidean
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        context.geometric_point = point
        context.squared_distance = distance
        context.gate.forward = gate
        with torch.no_grad():
            context.gamma_context.copy_(gamma)


def score(counts):
    tp, fp, fn, tn = counts
    return dict(tp=tp, fp=fp, fn=fn, tn=tn,
                F1=2*tp/max(2*tp+fp+fn, 1), IoU=tp/max(tp+fp+fn, 1),
                precision=tp/max(tp+fp, 1), recall=tp/max(tp+fn, 1))


def small_foreground(target):
    masks = []
    for sample in target[:, 0].cpu().numpy().astype(np.uint8):
        count, labels, stats, _ = cv2.connectedComponentsWithStats(sample, connectivity=8)
        selected = np.zeros_like(sample)
        for index in range(1, count):
            if stats[index, cv2.CC_STAT_AREA] <= 64:
                selected[labels == index] = 1
        masks.append(selected)
    return torch.as_tensor(np.stack(masks), device=target.device).bool()[:, None]


def analyze(model, loader, device):
    modes = ('baseline', 'context_off', 'fixed_radius', 'fixed_direction', 'gate_open', 'euclidean_metric')
    counts = {mode: [0, 0, 0, 0] for mode in modes}
    groups = {}
    captured_gate, captured_radius = [], []
    project_queries = model.context.project_queries
    def capture_queries(feature):
        query = project_queries(feature)
        captured_radius.append(torch.asinh(query[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1)).mean(1, keepdim=True))
        return query
    model.context.project_queries = capture_queries
    hooks = [model.context.gate.register_forward_hook(
        lambda module, inputs, output: captured_gate.append(output.sigmoid().mean(1, keepdim=True)))]
    diagnostics = None
    try:
        with torch.inference_mode():
            for image, target in loader:
                image, target = image.to(device).float(), target.to(device).float()
                predictions = {}
                for mode in modes:
                    captured_gate.clear()
                    captured_radius.clear()
                    model.context.collect_diag = mode == 'baseline' and diagnostics is None
                    with intervention(model.context, mode):
                        prediction = model.predict(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:]) > .5
                    if mode == 'baseline':
                        gates = [g.clone() for g in captured_gate]
                        radii = [radius.clone() for radius in captured_radius]
                        if diagnostics is None:
                            diagnostics = model.diagnostics()
                    truth = target > .5
                    batch_counts = [(prediction & truth).sum(), (prediction & ~truth).sum(),
                                    (~prediction & truth).sum(), (~prediction & ~truth).sum()]
                    counts[mode] = [a + int(b) for a, b in zip(counts[mode], batch_counts)]
                    predictions[mode] = prediction
                base, off = predictions['baseline'], predictions['context_off']
                masks = dict(true_change=truth, unchanged=~truth,
                             missed_without_context=truth & ~off,
                             false_positive_without_context=~truth & off,
                             corrected=(off != truth) & (base == truth),
                             harmed=(off == truth) & (base != truth),
                             boundary_1px=mask_boundary(target, 1).bool(),
                             small_foreground_le64=small_foreground(truth))
                for name, mask in masks.items():
                    group = groups.setdefault(name, dict(pixels=0, gate_sum=[0.]*4, radius_sum=[0.]*4))
                    group['pixels'] += int(mask.sum())
                    for index, (gate, radius) in enumerate(zip(gates, radii)):
                        gate = F.interpolate(gate, target.shape[-2:], mode='bilinear', align_corners=False)
                        radius = F.interpolate(radius, target.shape[-2:], mode='bilinear', align_corners=False)
                        group['gate_sum'][index] += float((gate * mask).sum())
                        group['radius_sum'][index] += float((radius * mask).sum())
    finally:
        model.context.project_queries = project_queries
        for hook in hooks:
            hook.remove()
        model.context.collect_diag = False
    for group in groups.values():
        count = group['pixels']
        group['mean_gate_by_stage'] = [x/count if count else None for x in group.pop('gate_sum')]
        group['mean_radius_by_stage'] = [x/count if count else None for x in group.pop('radius_sum')]
    return dict(interventions={mode: score(value) for mode, value in counts.items()},
                pixel_groups=groups, first_batch_diagnostics=diagnostics,
                radius_scope='Actual dense Lorentz queries: asinh(norm(Q_spatial)), averaged over heads at each stage; bilinear resize for pixel groups',
                intervention_definitions=dict(
                    fixed_radius='For each sample/head, set nonorigin positions to their mean intrinsic radius; origins stay at zero and the all-position mean is preserved. Each query stage and the joint key bank are equalized separately.',
                    fixed_direction='Preserve every projected norm and intrinsic radius; place all spatial directions on the first coordinate axis.'),
                interpretation='Frozen-weight sensitivity and pixel associations; not retrained ablations')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--checkpoint', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--dataset-root', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--module', choices=('hyperbolic','matched'), default='hyperbolic')
    parser.add_argument('--split', choices=('train', 'val'), default='val')
    parser.add_argument('--samples', type=int, default=128)
    parser.add_argument('--batch-size', type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument('--device', default='cuda:0')
    parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    state = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location='cpu', weights_only=False)
    model = CASANet(module=args.module)
    model.load_state_dict(state['state_dict'] if 'state_dict' in state else state)
    model = model.to(device).eval()
    source = make_loader(args.dataset_root, args.split, args.batch_size, 0).dataset
    # Training samples are inspected without random augmentation.
    if args.split == 'train':
        source.transform = make_loader(args.dataset_root, 'val', args.batch_size, 0).dataset.transform
    count = min(args.samples, len(source))
    indices = np.linspace(0, len(source)-1, count, dtype=int).tolist()
    loader = DataLoader(Subset(source, indices), batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=False)
    report = analyze(model, loader, device)
    report.update(dataset=str(args.dataset_root), split=args.split, sample_indices=indices,
                  threshold=.5, checkpoint=str(args.checkpoint), module=args.module,
                  compression="first_order_class_pooling", small_object_max_area=64)
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report['interventions']))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/analyze_selectivity_ablation.py
================================================================================
"""Zero-training ablation: does prototype *selection* or *value content* matter?

Three frozen-weight interventions on the same validation images (CPU, no training):

  A ``uniform_attention``  attention weights replaced by ones/L (values unchanged)
  B ``mean_value``         the 224 value vectors replaced by their mean (attention unchanged)
  C ``no_context``         the gated context residual ``gamma_s * U_s`` set to zero

Each arm is scored at threshold 0.5 and at its own best threshold on the same
images, so the comparison is not confounded by the operating point.

The second half reproduces the suspected cause of value redundancy: when a cell
holds almost no changed pixels the class membership is close to uniform, so the
change and background prototypes are nearly the same convex combination and
their values must be similar.  ``cos(P+, P-)`` and ``cos(V+, V-)`` are therefore
binned by the cell's changed-pixel fraction.
"""
import argparse
import contextlib
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader

from analyze_geometry_degeneracy import (DATASETS, DATASET_ROOT, MODELS as BASE_MODELS,
                                         load_checkpoint, subset_loader)
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import feature_regions

CHECKPOINT_ROOT = Path("/share_datasets/yqwang/checkpoints/CASA-CD")
MODELS = dict(BASE_MODELS)
MODELS["CTRL"] = dict(root=CHECKPOINT_ROOT / "CASA-MinorGoal/Run1/CTRL",
                      module="none", tar="reparam", decoder="reparam",
                      note="Run1 MinorGoal CTRL: no context module (reference)")
THRESHOLDS = np.linspace(0.05, 0.95, 91)


def counts_to_metrics(counts):
    tp, fp, fn, tn = (float(x) for x in counts)
    return dict(F1=100.0 * 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
                precision=100.0 * tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
                recall=100.0 * tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
                IoU=100.0 * tp / max(tp + fp + fn, 1))


@torch.inference_mode()
def run_pass(model, loader, limit):
    probabilities, targets = [], []
    for index, (image, target) in enumerate(loader):
        if index >= limit:
            break
        probabilities.append(model.predict(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:]).flatten())
        targets.append((target > 0.5).flatten())
    prob = torch.cat(probabilities)
    truth = torch.cat(targets)
    positive = ((prob > 0.5) & truth).sum()
    negative = ((prob > 0.5) & ~truth).sum()
    missed = ((prob <= 0.5) & truth).sum()
    true_negative = ((prob <= 0.5) & ~truth).sum()
    fixed = counts_to_metrics((positive, negative, missed, true_negative))
    best = dict(F1=-1.0)
    for threshold in THRESHOLDS:
        predicted = prob > float(threshold)
        counts = ((predicted & truth).sum(), (predicted & ~truth).sum(),
                  (~predicted & truth).sum(), (~predicted & ~truth).sum())
        score = counts_to_metrics(counts)
        if score["F1"] > best["F1"]:
            best = dict(score, threshold=float(threshold))
    return dict(at_0p5=fixed, best_threshold=best, images=len(targets))


@contextlib.contextmanager
def ablation(context, name):
    """Instance-level monkeypatch of one context module; nothing in models/ changes."""
    if name == "orig" or not isinstance(context, HyperbolicContext):
        yield
        return
    if name == "uniform_attention":
        original = context.attention_logits

        def uniform(query, key, support, query_scale):
            return torch.zeros_like(original(query, key, support, query_scale))

        context.attention_logits = uniform
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            context.attention_logits = original
    elif name == "rolled_attention":
        original = context.attention_logits

        def rolled(query, key, support, query_scale):
            logits = original(query, key, support, query_scale)
            return logits.roll(logits.shape[2] // 3, dims=2)

        context.attention_logits = rolled
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            context.attention_logits = original
    elif name == "mean_value":
        original = context.readout

        def mean_value(feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy):
            mean = values.mean(2, keepdim=True)
            return original(feature, probability, mean.expand_as(values), occupancy,
                            prototype_occupancy)

        context.readout = mean_value
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            context.readout = original
    elif name == "no_context":
        original = context.readout

        def no_context(feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy):
            out = original(feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy)
            return (torch.zeros_like(out[0]),) + tuple(out[1:])

        context.readout = no_context
        try:
            yield
        finally:
            context.readout = original
    else:
        raise ValueError(name)


@torch.inference_mode()
def capture_prototype_pairs(model, loader, limit):
    """Per-scale cos(P+, P-) and cos(V+, V-) against the cell's changed fraction."""
    context = model.context
    if not isinstance(context, HyperbolicContext):
        return None
    import model.layers.hyperbolic_context as module

    store = dict(prototypes=[], values=[], occupancy=[])
    original_pool = module.pool_features
    original_readout = context.readout

    def pool(feature, weights, grid):
        output = original_pool(feature, weights, grid)
        store["prototypes"].append(output.detach().float())
        return output

    def readout(feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy):
        store["values"].append(values.detach().float())
        store["occupancy"].append(occupancy.detach().float())
        return original_readout(feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy)

    module.pool_features = pool
    context.readout = readout
    labels = []
    try:
        for index, (image, target) in enumerate(loader):
            if index >= limit:
                break
            model.predict(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
            labels.append((target > 0.5).float())
    finally:
        module.pool_features = original_pool
        context.readout = original_readout

    scales = len(store["occupancy"]) // max(len(labels), 1)
    del scales
    scale_count = len(store["occupancy"]) // max(len(labels), 1)
    token_counts = [store["prototypes"][stage].shape[1] for stage in range(scale_count)]
    offsets = [sum(token_counts[:stage]) for stage in range(scale_count)]
    per_scale = []
    for stage in range(scale_count):
        cos_proto, cos_value, fraction_pred, fraction_truth = [], [], [], []
        for image in range(len(labels)):
            prototype = store["prototypes"][image * scale_count + stage]
            values = store["values"][image * scale_count + stage]
            occupancy = store["occupancy"][image * scale_count + stage]
            tokens, channels = prototype.shape[1], prototype.shape[2]
            cells = tokens // 2
            grid = int(round(cells ** 0.5))
            proto = prototype[0].reshape(cells, 2, channels)
            proto = torch.nn.functional.normalize(proto, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
            cos_proto.append((proto[:, 0] * proto[:, 1]).sum(-1))
            value = values[0].reshape(values.shape[1], -1, values.shape[-1]).mean(0)
            value = value[offsets[stage]:offsets[stage] + tokens].reshape(cells, 2, -1)
            value = torch.nn.functional.normalize(value, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
            cos_value.append((value[:, 0] * value[:, 1]).sum(-1))
            fraction_pred.append(
                feature_regions(occupancy, grid).squeeze(-1).mean(-1)[0])
            fraction_truth.append(
                feature_regions(labels[image], grid).squeeze(-1).mean(-1)[0])
        cos_proto = torch.cat(cos_proto).numpy()
        cos_value = torch.cat(cos_value).numpy()
        fraction_pred = torch.cat(fraction_pred).numpy()
        fraction_truth = torch.cat(fraction_truth).numpy()
        edges = [0.0, 0.01, 0.05, 0.20, 1.0]
        bins = []
        for low, high in zip(edges[:-1], edges[1:]):
            member = (fraction_truth >= low) & (fraction_truth < high)
            entry = dict(bin=f"[{low:.2f},{high:.2f})", count=int(member.sum()))
            if int(member.sum()) >= 8:
                entry.update(cos_proto_p50=float(np.percentile(cos_proto[member], 50)),
                             cos_proto_p95=float(np.percentile(cos_proto[member], 95)),
                             cos_value_p50=float(np.percentile(cos_value[member], 50)),
                             cos_value_p95=float(np.percentile(cos_value[member], 95)))
            bins.append(entry)
        per_scale.append(dict(
            scale=stage + 1, grid=grid, cells=int(cells),
            cos_proto=dict(p50=float(np.percentile(cos_proto, 50)),
                           p95=float(np.percentile(cos_proto, 95))),
            cos_value=dict(p50=float(np.percentile(cos_value, 50)),
                           p95=float(np.percentile(cos_value, 95))),
            bins_by_truth_fraction=bins,
            predicted_fraction_p50=float(np.percentile(fraction_pred, 50)),
            truth_fraction_p50=float(np.percentile(fraction_truth, 50))))
    return dict(scales=per_scale, images=len(labels),
                note="cosine of change vs background prototype (P) and value (V), "
                     "binned by the cell's ground-truth changed-pixel fraction")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--models", default="DIFF,SPP,CTRL")
    parser.add_argument("--datasets", default="WHU")
    parser.add_argument("--images", type=int, default=200)
    parser.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=8)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    torch.set_num_threads(args.threads)
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    report = dict(meta=dict(script="models/analyze_selectivity_ablation.py", device="cpu",
                            images=args.images, threshold=0.5,
                            thresholds=f"{THRESHOLDS[0]:.2f}..{THRESHOLDS[-1]:.2f}",
                            arms=list(("orig", "uniform_attention", "rolled_attention", "mean_value",
                                      "no_context")),
                            note="frozen weights; instance-level monkeypatching only"),
                  results={})
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name in [x for x in args.models.split(",") if x]:
        spec = MODELS[name]
        for dataset in [x for x in args.datasets.split(",") if x]:
            model, path = load_checkpoint(spec, dataset)
            loader, indices = subset_loader(DATASET_ROOT / DATASETS[dataset], "val", args.images)
            entry = dict(checkpoint=path, module=spec["module"], note=spec["note"],
                         val_images=len(indices), arms={})
            applicable = isinstance(model.context, HyperbolicContext)
            entry["context_branch"] = bool(applicable)
            arms = ("orig", "uniform_attention", "rolled_attention", "mean_value",
                    "no_context") if applicable \
                else ("orig",)
            for arm in arms:
                started = time.time()
                with ablation(model.context, arm):
                    entry["arms"][arm] = run_pass(model, loader, args.images)
                entry["arms"][arm]["seconds"] = round(time.time() - started, 1)
                print(f"[{name}/{dataset}/{arm}] F1@0.5="
                      f"{entry['arms'][arm]['at_0p5']['F1']:.4f} best="
                      f"{entry['arms'][arm]['best_threshold']['F1']:.4f}", flush=True)
            if applicable:
                baseline = entry["arms"]["orig"]
                for arm in arms[1:]:
                    entry["arms"][arm]["delta_f1_at_0p5"] = \
                        entry["arms"][arm]["at_0p5"]["F1"] - baseline["at_0p5"]["F1"]
                    entry["arms"][arm]["delta_f1_best"] = \
                        entry["arms"][arm]["best_threshold"]["F1"] - baseline["best_threshold"]["F1"]
                entry["pairs"] = capture_prototype_pairs(model, loader, args.images)
            report["results"].setdefault(name, {})[dataset] = entry
            args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
            del model
    print("[DONE]", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/check_per_image_metrics.py
================================================================================
"""Verify that --per-image-metrics does not move a single published number.

Runs the same frozen checkpoint twice on the same fixed test subset: once with the
plain ``evaluate`` call and once with the per-image collector, then compares the
returned score dictionaries entry by entry and the collected TP/FP/FN totals
against the confusion matrix.
"""
import argparse

import numpy as np
import torch

from analyze_geometry_degeneracy import DATASET_ROOT, DATASETS, MODELS, load_checkpoint, subset_loader
from train_CASA import evaluate


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--model", default="DIFF", choices=sorted(MODELS))
    parser.add_argument("--dataset", default="WHU", choices=sorted(DATASETS))
    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=32)
    parser.add_argument("--threads", type=int, default=4)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    torch.set_num_threads(args.threads)
    device = torch.device("cpu")
    model, path = load_checkpoint(MODELS[args.model], args.dataset)
    root = DATASET_ROOT / DATASETS[args.dataset]
    loader, indices = subset_loader(root, "test", args.limit)
    plain = evaluate(model, loader, device)[1]

    loader, _ = subset_loader(root, "test", args.limit)
    rows = []
    with_rows = evaluate(model, loader, device, per_image=rows)[1]

    assert list(plain) == list(with_rows), (list(plain), list(with_rows))
    drift = {key: float(with_rows[key]) - float(plain[key]) for key in plain}
    assert all(abs(value) == 0.0 for value in drift.values()), drift

    table = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)
    print(f"[CHECK] {path} {args.dataset} test[:{args.limit}] drifts all zero")
    print(f"[CHECK] rows={len(table)} tp={int(table[:, 1].sum())} "
          f"fp={int(table[:, 2].sum())} fn={int(table[:, 3].sum())} images={indices}")
    print(f"[CHECK] scores={ {key: round(float(value), 4) for key, value in plain.items()} }")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/check_run3_switches.py
================================================================================
"""Fail-fast checks for the Run3 step-A/B switches (kv-hierarchy / support / freeze-tau).

CPU only: no checkpoint, no dataset, no GPU memory. Checks:
  1. nested parent/child index structure per scale pair;
  2. forward + backward through all four scales (no NaN, q/k gradients alive);
  3. deploy folding maxdiff (flat and nested) and binary agreement;
  4. trainable/deploy parameter counts for the four hierarchy x support combos;
  5. KV token counts (flat 224, nested 170);
  6. nested parent-to-child pooling actually changes the update;
  7. --support pixel really changes the support tensor and logits under identical weights;
  8. --freeze-tau removes the temperature gradient;
  9. the difference context accepts (difference + spp) and (difference + diff, nested).
"""
import itertools
import math
import sys

import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F

sys.path.insert(0, ".")

from model.casa_net import CASANet  # noqa: E402
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import (  # noqa: E402
    FLAT_GRIDS, FROZEN_TAU, NESTED_GRIDS, HyperbolicContext)

EXPECTED = {  # director's Run3 table: hyperbolic + spp, flat == nested
    "trainable": 2129577, "deploy": 2006998,
}


def build(hierarchy, support, seed=2334):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    return CASANet(None, module="hyperbolic", context_seed=seed, tar_variant="spp",
                   support=support, kv_hierarchy=hierarchy).eval()


def counts(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


def fold_params(model):
    """Deploy parameter count without mutating the caller's model."""
    import copy
    clone = copy.deepcopy(model)
    with torch.no_grad():
        clone.switch_to_deploy()
    return sum(p.numel() for p in clone.parameters())


def check_hierarchy():
    grids = NESTED_GRIDS
    for parent_grid, child_grid in zip(grids[:-1], grids[1:]):
        assert child_grid * 2 == parent_grid, (parent_grid, child_grid)
        for row in range(child_grid):
            for col in range(child_grid):
                block = [(2 * row + dr) * parent_grid + (2 * col + dc)
                         for dr in (0, 1) for dc in (0, 1)]
                assert len(block) == 4 and len(set(block)) == 4, (row, col, block)
    print(f"[HIERARCHY] ok grids={grids} parent blocks are exact 2x2 neighbourhoods")


def check_forward_backward(model, tag):
    torch.manual_seed(7)
    a, b = torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256), torch.randn(2, 3, 256, 256)
    model.train()
    optimizer = torch.optim.SGD([p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad], lr=1e-3)
    # Step 1: gamma_context is zero-initialised, so at initialisation only the gate
    # receives gradient; that is by design and must be reported, not asserted away.
    loss = sum(o.float().mean() for o in model(a, b))
    loss.backward()
    assert torch.isfinite(loss).item(), f"{tag}: non-finite loss"
    q_at_init = model.context.qk.weight.grad.norm().item()
    gate = model.context.gamma_context.grad.norm().item()
    assert gate > 0, f"{tag}: gate gradient is zero"
    optimizer.step()
    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    with torch.no_grad():
        model.context.gamma_context.add_(0.1)
    # Step 2: with the gate off zero the q/k projection must be trainable.
    loss = sum(o.float().mean() for o in model(a, b))
    loss.backward()
    q_norm = model.context.qk.weight.grad.norm().item()
    v_grad = model.context.v.weight.grad
    v_norm = 0.0 if v_grad is None else v_grad.norm().item()
    assert q_norm > 0, f"{tag}: q/k projection gradient is zero after one gate step"
    assert v_norm > 0, f"{tag}: value projection gradient is zero"
    grads = [p.grad for p in model.parameters() if p.grad is not None]
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() for g in grads), f"{tag}: non-finite gradients"
    print(f"[GRAD] {tag} loss={loss.item():.6f} qk_grad@{tag}={q_norm:.3e} "
          f"v_grad={v_norm:.3e} qk_grad_at_init={q_at_init:.3e} gate_grad={gate:.3e}")
    return {"loss": loss.item(), "qk_grad": q_norm, "v_grad": v_norm,
            "qk_grad_at_init": q_at_init, "gate_grad": gate}


def check_fold(hierarchy):
    model = build(hierarchy, "cell")
    torch.manual_seed(11)
    a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256), torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256)
    with torch.no_grad():
        before = model.predict(a, b).clone()
        model.switch_to_deploy()
        after = model.predict(a, b)
    maxdiff = (before - after).abs().max().item()
    binary = int((before > .5).ne(after > .5).sum().item())
    assert maxdiff <= 2e-4, f"{hierarchy}: fold maxdiff {maxdiff:.3e}"
    assert binary == 0, f"{hierarchy}: {binary} flipped pixels after fold"
    print(f"[FOLD] {hierarchy} maxdiff={maxdiff:.3e} binary_diff={binary} pixels={before.numel()}")
    return {"maxdiff": maxdiff, "binary_diff": binary}


def check_support_effect():
    """Same weights, different support prior: the support tensor and logits must differ."""
    mass = build("flat", "cell")
    pixel = build("flat", "pixel")
    pixel.load_state_dict(mass.state_dict())
    torch.manual_seed(13)
    a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256), torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256)
    captured = {}

    def capture(context, tag):
        original = context.attention_logits

        def wrapped(query, key, support, support_index, *args, **kwargs):
            recorded = captured.setdefault(tag, [])
            recorded.append(support.detach().clone())
            out = original(query, key, support, support_index, *args, **kwargs)
            captured[f"{tag}_logits"] = captured.get(f"{tag}_logits", [])
            captured[f"{tag}_logits"].append(out.detach().clone())
            return out
        context.attention_logits = wrapped

    capture(mass.context, "mass")
    capture(pixel.context, "pixel")
    with torch.no_grad():
        mass.predict(a, b)
        pixel.predict(a, b)
    support_diff = max((x - y).abs().max().item()
                       for x, y in zip(captured["mass"], captured["pixel"]))
    logit_diff = max((x - y).abs().max().item()
                     for x, y in zip(captured["mass_logits"], captured["pixel_logits"]))
    assert support_diff > 1e-4, "support pixel is a silent no-op (support unchanged)"
    assert logit_diff > 1e-4, "support pixel does not change the attention logits"
    print(f"[SUPPORT] support maxdiff={support_diff:.3e} logits maxdiff={logit_diff:.3e}")
    return {"support_maxdiff": support_diff, "logits_maxdiff": logit_diff}


def check_freeze_tau():
    frozen = build("flat", "cell")
    frozen_fold = CASANet(None, module="hyperbolic", context_seed=2334, tar_variant="spp",
                          support="cell", freeze_tau=True).eval()
    tau_frozen = F.softplus(frozen_fold.context.raw_temperature.detach()).mean().item()
    assert not frozen_fold.context.raw_temperature.requires_grad
    assert abs(tau_frozen - FROZEN_TAU) < 1e-4, tau_frozen
    delta = (sum(p.numel() for p in frozen.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
             - sum(p.numel() for p in frozen_fold.parameters() if p.requires_grad))
    assert delta == 1, delta
    assert sum(p.numel() for p in frozen_fold.parameters()) == sum(
        p.numel() for p in frozen.parameters()), "freeze_tau must not change the parameter count"
    print(f"[TAU] frozen softplus(tau)={tau_frozen:.6f} (target {FROZEN_TAU}) params delta={delta}")
    return {"tau": tau_frozen, "params_delta": delta}


def check_nested_effect():
    """Same weights: nested must change the context output, and pooling must be the 2x2 mean."""
    flat = build("flat", "cell")
    nested = build("nested", "cell")
    nested.load_state_dict(flat.state_dict())
    context = nested.context
    batch, dim = 1, 3
    prototypes = []
    for grid in NESTED_GRIDS:
        cells = 2 * grid * grid
        base = torch.arange(batch * cells * dim, dtype=torch.float32).reshape(batch, cells, dim)
        prototypes.append(base)
    aggregated = context.aggregate_nested(prototypes)
    parent = prototypes[0].reshape(batch, 8, 8, 2, dim)
    pooled = parent.reshape(batch, 4, 2, 4, 2, 2, dim).mean(dim=(2, 4)).reshape(batch, -1, dim)
    expected = 0.5 * (prototypes[1] + pooled)
    maxdiff = (aggregated[1] - expected).abs().max().item()
    assert maxdiff < 1e-6, f"nested pooling is not the 2x2 parent mean: {maxdiff}"
    torch.manual_seed(17)
    a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256), torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256)
    updates = {}

    def capture(context, tag):
        original = context.readout

        def wrapped(*args, **kwargs):
            out = original(*args, **kwargs)
            updates.setdefault(tag, []).append(out[0].detach().clone())
            return out
        context.readout = wrapped

    capture(flat.context, "flat")
    capture(nested.context, "nested")
    with torch.no_grad():
        flat.context.gamma_context.fill_(1.0)
        nested.context.gamma_context.fill_(1.0)
        out_flat = flat.predict(a, b)
        out_nested = nested.predict(a, b)
    update_diff = max((x - y).abs().max().item()
                      for x, y in zip(updates["flat"], updates["nested"]))
    assert update_diff > 1e-4, f"nested hierarchy leaves the readout update unchanged: {update_diff}"
    diff = (out_flat - out_nested).abs().max().item()
    print(f"[NESTED] pooling maxdiff={maxdiff:.3e} update maxdiff={update_diff:.3e} "
          f"output maxdiff={diff:.3e} (gamma=1)")
    return {"pooling_maxdiff": maxdiff, "update_maxdiff": update_diff, "output_maxdiff": diff}


def check_difference_passthrough():
    torch.manual_seed(2334)
    model = CASANet(None, module="difference", context_seed=2334, tar_variant="spp",
                    kv_hierarchy="nested", support="pixel").eval()
    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
    deploy = fold_params(model)
    assert model.context.grid_sizes == NESTED_GRIDS, model.context.grid_sizes
    assert (trainable, deploy) == (2129705, 2007126), (trainable, deploy)
    print(f"[DIFF-NESTED] grids={model.context.grid_sizes} trainable={trainable} deploy={deploy}")


def main():
    report = {"hierarchy": {}, "params": {}, "fold": {}, "kv": {}}
    check_hierarchy()
    for hierarchy in ("flat", "nested"):
        model = build(hierarchy, "cell")
        grids = model.context.grid_sizes
        kv = sum(2 * g * g for g in grids)
        report["kv"][hierarchy] = kv
        print(f"[KV] {hierarchy} grids={grids} kv={kv} context_grids={model.context.grid_sizes}")
        report["hierarchy"][hierarchy] = check_forward_backward(model, hierarchy)
        report["fold"][hierarchy] = check_fold(hierarchy)
    for hierarchy, support in itertools.product(("flat", "nested"), ("cell", "pixel")):
        model = build(hierarchy, support)
        trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
        deploy = fold_params(model)
        report["params"][f"{hierarchy}/{support}"] = {"trainable": trainable, "deploy": deploy}
        print(f"[PARAMS] {hierarchy}/{support} trainable={trainable} deploy={deploy}")
        assert trainable == EXPECTED["trainable"], (hierarchy, support, trainable)
        assert deploy == EXPECTED["deploy"], (hierarchy, support, deploy)
    report["support"] = check_support_effect()
    report["tau"] = check_freeze_tau()
    report["nested"] = check_nested_effect()
    check_difference_passthrough()
    assert report["kv"] == {"flat": 224, "nested": 170}, report["kv"]
    print("[OK] all Run3 switch checks passed")
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/operating_point.py
================================================================================
"""Exact pixel counts at a fixed validation threshold grid."""
import numpy as np


THRESHOLDS = np.linspace(.10, .70, 121, dtype=np.float32)
DEFAULT_INDEX = int(np.searchsorted(THRESHOLDS, .5))


class ThresholdCounts:
    def __init__(self, thresholds=THRESHOLDS):
        self.thresholds = np.asarray(thresholds, dtype=np.float32)
        self.histogram = np.zeros((2, len(self.thresholds) + 1), dtype=np.int64)

    def update(self, probabilities, targets):
        bins = np.searchsorted(self.thresholds, probabilities, side='left')
        joint = bins.ravel() + self.histogram.shape[1] * targets.ravel().astype(np.int64)
        self.histogram += np.bincount(joint, minlength=self.histogram.size).reshape(self.histogram.shape)

    def curve(self):
        fp, tp = np.cumsum(self.histogram[:, :0:-1], axis=1)[:, ::-1]
        fn = self.histogram[1].sum() - tp
        tn = self.histogram[0].sum() - fp
        return [dict(threshold=round(float(threshold), 3), tp=int(t), fp=int(f), fn=int(n), tn=int(k),
                     F1=float(2*t / max(2*t+f+n, 1)), recall=float(t / max(t+n, 1)),
                     precision=float(t / max(t+f, 1)))
                for threshold, t, f, n, k in zip(self.thresholds, tp, fp, fn, tn)]

    def summary(self):
        curve = self.curve()
        best = max(curve, key=lambda point: (point['F1'], -abs(point['threshold'] - .5)))
        return dict(default=curve[DEFAULT_INDEX], best=best, curve=curve)

================================================================================
# FILE: models/profile_CASA.py
================================================================================
"""Profile CASA variants: training memory (legacy), deployed GFLOPs, and latency.

Modes
-----
memory   (default) full-batch training memory/throughput, as before.
flops    parameter count + GFLOPs at 1x3x256x256, eval, after switch_to_deploy().
latency  batch-1 fp32 inference latency (needs a free GPU).
all      flops + latency in one process.

FLOPs are cross-checked with two independent counters:
  * torch.utils.flop_counter.FlopCounterMode
  * fvcore.nn.FlopCountAnalysis
A >2% disagreement is reported so the caller can inspect uncounted ops
(e.g. F.interpolate / upsample is not a FLOP-counted kernel in fvcore).
"""
import argparse
import gc
import json
import os
import platform
import subprocess
import time
from pathlib import Path

import torch
from torch.utils.flop_counter import FlopCounterMode

from model.casa_net import CASANet

# Run1 / Run3 arm table.  Params are checked against the published deployment
# counts (CTRL 1,935,810 / SPP 1,995,714 / DIFF 2,007,126).
ARMS = {
    "CTRL": dict(module="none", tar="reparam", decoder="reparam", trained=True,
                 checkpoint="Run1/CTRL"),
    "SPP": dict(module="none", tar="spp", decoder="reparam", trained=True,
                checkpoint="Run1/SPP"),
    "DIFF": dict(module="difference", tar="spp", decoder="reparam", trained=True,
                 checkpoint="Run1/DIFF"),
    # ARM-C is the parameter-matched content control (hyperbolic + spp); no
    # trained checkpoint exists, architecture/FLOPs are what matter here.
    "ARM-C": dict(module="hyperbolic", tar="spp", decoder="reparam", trained=False,
                  checkpoint=None),
    # ARM-D (difference_matched) is not implemented in models/model, so it
    # cannot be instantiated; it is reported as unavailable in the JSON.
    "ARM-D": dict(module=None, tar="spp", decoder="reparam", trained=False,
                  checkpoint=None),
    # DETAIL is Run3's boundary arm: DIFF plus the zero-initialised detail head,
    # which stays in the deployment graph.
    "DETAIL": dict(module="difference", tar="spp", decoder="reparam", trained=False,
                   checkpoint=None, detail_head=True),
}


def build_model(spec, device, pretrained=None):
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    model = CASANet(pretrained, spec["module"], tar_variant=spec["tar"],
                    decoder_variant=spec["decoder"], context_heads=1,
                    detail_head=spec.get("detail_head", False))
    return model.eval().to(device)


def param_count(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


def deploy_fold(model, device):
    """switch_to_deploy() and verify the folded graph matches the original.

    Runs under no_grad(), not inference_mode(): folding materialises new
    parameters, and inference tensors stored as parameters break fvcore's
    jit trace with "Inference tensors cannot be saved for backward".
    """
    a = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    b = torch.randn_like(a)
    with torch.no_grad():
        before = model.predict(a, b).clone()
        model.switch_to_deploy()
        after = model.predict(a, b)
    return dict(maxdiff=(before - after).abs().max().item(),
                binary_diff=int(((before > 0.5) != (after > 0.5)).sum()),
                pixels=int(after.numel()))


def _sum_nested(node):
    """FlopCounterMode.get_flop_counts() nests module -> op -> shapes -> count."""
    if isinstance(node, dict):
        return sum(_sum_nested(value) for value in node.values())
    return float(node)


def measure_head_mac(model, device):
    """Head cost at its real input resolution (the decoder's finest scale)."""
    shapes = []

    def hook(module, inputs):
        shapes.append(tuple(inputs[0].shape))

    handle = model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(hook)
    try:
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.predict(torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256, device=device),
                          torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256, device=device))
    finally:
        handle.remove()
    shape = shapes[0] if shapes else (1, model.head.in_channels, 64, 64)
    mac = shape[2] * shape[3] * model.head.in_channels
    return float(mac), list(shape)


def count_flops(model, device):
    a = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    b = torch.randn_like(a)
    with torch.inference_mode():
        with FlopCounterMode(display=False) as counter:
            model.predict(a, b)
    torch_total = float(counter.get_total_flops())
    torch_by = {k: _sum_nested(v) for k, v in counter.get_flop_counts().items()}
    head_mac, head_shape = measure_head_mac(model, device)

    class _Single(torch.nn.Module):
        def __init__(self, wrapped):
            super().__init__()
            self.wrapped = wrapped

        def forward(self, x, y):
            return self.wrapped.predict(x, y)

    wrapper = _Single(model)
    try:
        from fvcore.nn import FlopCountAnalysis
        analysis = FlopCountAnalysis(wrapper, (a, b))
        analysis.unsupported_ops_warnings(False)
        if hasattr(analysis, "uncalled_modules_warnings"):
            analysis.uncalled_modules_warnings(False)
        fv_total = float(analysis.total())
        fv_by = {k: float(v) for k, v in analysis.by_module().items()}
        # fvcore counts one fused multiply-add as one FLOP; disable the
        # elementwise/BN/upsample handles so the remaining number is pure
        # conv/matmul MACs, directly comparable to torch_counter/2.
        for op in ("aten::batch_norm", "aten::group_norm", "aten::layer_norm",
                   "aten::instance_norm", "aten::upsample_nearest2d",
                   "aten::upsample_bilinear2d", "aten::adaptive_avg_pool2d",
                   "aten::grid_sampler"):
            analysis.set_op_handle(op, None)
        fv_mac = float(analysis.total())
        fv_error = None
    except Exception as error:  # fvcore can choke on dynamic ops; keep the torch number
        fv_total, fv_by, fv_mac, fv_error = None, {}, None, f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"

    torch_half = torch_total / 2.0
    if fv_mac is None:
        diff_pct = None
    else:
        diff_pct = abs(torch_half - fv_mac) / max(torch_half, fv_mac) * 100
    return dict(
        # convention A: papers/thop style, one fused multiply-add = 1 FLOP
        mac_gflops=None if fv_mac is None else fv_mac / 1e9,
        # convention B: torch FlopCounterMode, multiply and add counted apart
        flop2_gflops=torch_total / 1e9,
        torch_counter_gflops=torch_total / 1e9,
        fvcore_gflops=None if fv_total is None else fv_total / 1e9,
        cross_check_pct=diff_pct,
        cross_check_rule="|torch_counter/2 - fvcore(conv+matmul only)| / max < 2%",
        fvcore_error=fv_error,
        head_mac_gflops=head_mac / 1e9,
        head_input_shape=head_shape,
        head_share_pct=None if fv_mac is None else head_mac / fv_mac * 100,
        no_head_mac_gflops=None if fv_mac is None else (fv_mac - head_mac) / 1e9,
        by_module_torch={k: v / 1e9 for k, v in torch_by.items() if v},
        by_module_fvcore={k: v / 1e9 for k, v in fv_by.items() if v},
    )


def _percentile(values, q):
    ordered = sorted(values)
    if not ordered:
        return None
    position = (len(ordered) - 1) * q / 100.0
    lower = int(position)
    upper = min(lower + 1, len(ordered) - 1)
    return ordered[lower] + (ordered[upper] - ordered[lower]) * (position - lower)


def bench_latency(model, device, warmup=100, iters=1000):
    model.eval()
    a = torch.randn(1, 3, 256, 256, device=device)
    b = torch.randn_like(a)
    if device.type != "cuda":
        times = []
        with torch.inference_mode():
            for _ in range(warmup):
                model.predict(a, b)
            for _ in range(iters):
                start = time.perf_counter()
                model.predict(a, b)
                times.append((time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000.0)
        return dict(n=len(times), warmup=warmup, device=str(device),
                    median_ms=_percentile(times, 50), p90_ms=_percentile(times, 90),
                    mean_ms=sum(times) / len(times), min_ms=min(times), max_ms=max(times))
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
    with torch.inference_mode():
        for _ in range(warmup):
            model.predict(a, b)
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
        starts = [torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True) for _ in range(iters)]
        ends = [torch.cuda.Event(enable_timing=True) for _ in range(iters)]
        for index in range(iters):
            starts[index].record()
            model.predict(a, b)
            ends[index].record()
        torch.cuda.synchronize()
    times = [s.elapsed_time(e) for s, e in zip(starts, ends)]
    return dict(n=len(times), warmup=warmup, device=str(device),
                median_ms=_percentile(times, 50), p90_ms=_percentile(times, 90),
                mean_ms=sum(times) / len(times), min_ms=min(times), max_ms=max(times),
                std_ms=(sum((t - sum(times) / len(times)) ** 2 for t in times) / len(times)) ** 0.5)


def wait_for_idle_gpu(mem_mib, util_pct, timeout_s, poll_s=60, only=None):
    """Block until at least one GPU is idle enough not to perturb a co-tenant run.

    Used while Run2 owns both 5090s; the benchmark then runs on the first GPU
    that frees up (Run2 keeps two slots per card, so a card only counts as free
    once its memory drops to the idle footprint).
    """
    deadline = time.time() + timeout_s
    while True:
        rows = subprocess.check_output(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=index,memory.used,utilization.gpu",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"], text=True).strip().splitlines()
        free = []
        for row in rows:
            index, used, util = (part.strip() for part in row.split(","))
            if only is not None and int(index) not in only:
                continue
            if float(used) <= mem_mib and float(util) <= util_pct:
                free.append(int(index))
        if free:
            return free[0], rows
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            raise TimeoutError(f"no idle GPU within {timeout_s}s: {rows}")
        print(f"[WAIT] {time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')} busy: {rows}", flush=True)
        time.sleep(poll_s)


def gpu_identity(index):
    props = torch.cuda.get_device_properties(index)
    out = dict(index=index, name=props.name, total_memory_mib=props.total_memory / 1024**2,
               capability=f"{props.major}.{props.minor}",
               uuid=str(getattr(props, "uuid", "")))
    try:
        row = subprocess.check_output(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=index,uuid,name,clocks.sm,clocks.mem,power.draw",
             "--format=csv,noheader,nounits", "-i", str(index)], text=True).strip()
        out["nvidia_smi"] = row
    except Exception as error:
        out["nvidia_smi_error"] = str(error)
    return out


def run_memory(args):
    from train_CASA import (PROTOCOL, GUIDE_OBJECTIVE, guide_effective_coefficients,
                            auxiliary_weights, make_loader, training_losses, make_optimizer)
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    image, target = next(iter(make_loader(args.dataset_root, "train")))
    image, target = image.cuda().float(), target.cuda().float()
    results = {}
    for variant in args.modules:
        torch.manual_seed(2333)
        model = CASANet(args.pretrained, variant, context_heads=args.context_heads,
                        detail_head=args.detail_head, detail_target=args.detail_target,
                        tar_variant=args.tar_variant, decoder_variant=args.decoder_variant).cuda().train()
        optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats()
        times = []
        for _ in range(6):
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            start = time.time()
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits, auxiliary = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
            loss, main_loss, auxiliary_loss = training_losses(logits, auxiliary, target, args.guide_weight, args.retrieval_weight, args.interior_positive_weight)
            loss.backward()
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), float("inf"), error_if_nonfinite=True, foreach=True)
            optimizer.step()
            torch.cuda.synchronize()
            times.append(time.time() - start)
        model.context.collect_diag = True
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.predict(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
        used = subprocess.check_output(
            ["nvidia-smi", "--query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
            text=True)
        process_mib = next(float(row.split(",")[1]) for row in used.splitlines()
                           if int(row.split(",")[0]) == os.getpid())
        context_mib = process_mib - torch.cuda.memory_reserved() / 1024**2
        result = dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, batch_size=image.shape[0],
                      training_outputs=4, training_prediction_format="logits",
                      guide_objective=GUIDE_OBJECTIVE if variant in ("hyperbolic", "matched") else "raw_area_bce",
                      guide_effective_coefficients=guide_effective_coefficients(variant, auxiliary_weights(variant, args.guide_weight, args.retrieval_weight)["guide"]),
                      auxiliary_weights=auxiliary_weights(variant, args.guide_weight, args.retrieval_weight),
                      compression="first_order_class_pooling" if variant in ("hyperbolic", "matched") else "none", encoder_lr_mult=1.0,
                      inference_method="predict", inference_outputs=1,
                      grid_sizes=list(model.context.grid_sizes),
                      contexts_per_scale=list(model.context.tokens_per_scale),
                      joint_contexts=sum(model.context.tokens_per_scale),
                      initial_compression_power=model.context.compression_power,
                      interior_positive_weight=args.interior_positive_weight,
                      detail_head=args.detail_head,
                      compression_power=model.context.compression_power,
                      support_power=model.context.support_power,
                      self_reference=model.context.self_reference,
                      qk_geometry=model.context.geometry, value_geometry=model.context.value_geometry,
                      heads=model.context.heads if variant != "none" else 0,
                      qkv_dim=model.context.qk.out_channels if variant != "none" else 0,
                      hyperbolic_radius=model.context.radius_mode if variant in ("hyperbolic", "matched") else None,
                      context_projection_weight_decay=1e-4, context_projection_weight_decay_mode="decoupled",
                      body_weight_decay_mode="coupled_l2", context_other_weight_decay=0.0,
                      optimizer_groups=[dict(name=g["group_name"], weight_decay=g["weight_decay"],
                                             decoupled_weight_decay=g["decoupled_weight_decay"])
                                        for g in optimizer.param_groups],
                      peak_allocated_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated() / 1024**2,
                      peak_reserved_mib=torch.cuda.max_memory_reserved() / 1024**2,
                      context_overhead_mib=context_mib, process_memory_mib=process_mib,
                      seconds_per_step=sum(times[2:]) / len(times[2:]), loss=float(loss.detach()),
                      params=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()), diagnostics=model.diagnostics())
        results[variant] = result
        print(f"[PROFILE] {variant} {json.dumps(result)}", flush=True)
        del model, optimizer, logits, auxiliary, loss, main_loss, auxiliary_loss
        gc.collect()
        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")


def run_flops_latency(args):
    device = torch.device(args.device)
    if args.wait_idle and device.type == "cuda":
        print(f"[WAIT] looking for an idle GPU (mem<={args.idle_mem_mib} MiB, "
              f"util<={args.idle_util}%), timeout {args.wait_idle}s", flush=True)
        only = None if args.wait_gpu is None else {args.wait_gpu}
        index, rows = wait_for_idle_gpu(args.idle_mem_mib, args.idle_util, args.wait_idle,
                                        only=only)
        device = torch.device(f"cuda:{index}")
        print(f"[WAIT] using cuda:{index}; snapshot={rows}", flush=True)
    elif args.wait_idle and device.type == "cpu":
        print("[WAIT] --wait-idle ignored on cpu device", flush=True)
    report = dict(
        meta=dict(created=time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"), script="models/profile_CASA.py",
                  torch=torch.__version__, python=platform.python_version(),
                  device=str(device), input="1x3x256x256", dtype="float32",
                  mode="eval+switch_to_deploy", batch_size=1,
                  flops_counters=["torch.utils.flop_counter.FlopCounterMode",
                                  "fvcore.nn.FlopCountAnalysis"],
                  cudnn_benchmark=bool(torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark),
                  notes=["GFLOPs exclude upsample/activation kernels unless the counter lists them",
                         "ARM-D is not implemented in models/model (no difference_matched context)"]),
        arms={})
    if device.type == "cuda":
        report["meta"]["gpu"] = gpu_identity(device.index or 0)
    last = None
    for name in args.arms:
        spec = ARMS[name]
        if spec["module"] is None:
            report["arms"][name] = dict(available=False,
                                        reason="module 'difference_matched' not implemented in models/model")
            print(f"[PROFILE] {name} unavailable: {report['arms'][name]['reason']}", flush=True)
            continue
        model = build_model(spec, device)
        params = param_count(model)
        fold = deploy_fold(model, device)
        params_deploy = param_count(model)
        entry = dict(available=True, module=spec["module"], tar_variant=spec["tar"],
                     decoder_variant=spec["decoder"], trained=spec["trained"],
                     checkpoint=spec["checkpoint"], params=params,
                     params_deploy=params_deploy, deploy_fold=fold)
        if args.mode in ("flops", "all"):
            entry["flops"] = count_flops(model, device)
            flops = entry["flops"]
            print(f"[FLOPS] {name:6s} params={params:,} deploy={params_deploy:,} "
                  f"MAC={flops['mac_gflops']} GFLOP(2x)={flops['flop2_gflops']:.4f} "
                  f"cross={flops['cross_check_pct']} head={flops['head_mac_gflops']:.5f} "
                  f"fold_maxdiff={fold['maxdiff']:.3g}", flush=True)
        if args.mode in ("latency", "all"):
            latency = bench_latency(model, device, args.warmup, args.iters)
            entry["latency"] = latency
            gflops = entry.get("flops", {}).get("mac_gflops")
            if gflops:
                entry["effective_tflops"] = gflops / (latency["median_ms"] / 1000.0) / 1000.0
            print(f"[LATENCY] {name:6s} median={latency['median_ms']:.4f}ms "
                  f"p90={latency['p90_ms']:.4f}ms n={latency['n']}", flush=True)
        report["arms"][name] = entry
        last = model
        del model
        gc.collect()
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    del last
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[PROFILE] wrote {args.output}", flush=True)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=("memory", "flops", "latency", "all"), default="memory")
    parser.add_argument("--arms", nargs="+", choices=tuple(ARMS),
                        default=("CTRL", "SPP", "DIFF", "ARM-C", "ARM-D", "DETAIL"))
    parser.add_argument("--device", default="cpu")
    parser.add_argument("--warmup", type=int, default=100)
    parser.add_argument("--iters", type=int, default=1000)
    parser.add_argument("--wait-gpu", type=int, default=None,
                        help="restrict --wait-idle to this physical GPU index "
                             "(e.g. 1 = GPU1 only, GPU0 reserved for other users)")
    parser.add_argument("--wait-idle", type=float, default=0.0,
                        help="seconds to wait for an idle GPU before latency (0 = fail fast)")
    parser.add_argument("--idle-mem-mib", type=float, default=2000.0)
    parser.add_argument("--idle-util", type=float, default=10.0)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, required=True)
    # legacy training-memory options
    parser.add_argument("--dataset-root", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--pretrained")
    parser.add_argument("--modules", nargs="+", choices=("none", "hyperbolic", "qkv", "matched", "difference"),
                        default=("none", "hyperbolic", "matched"))
    parser.add_argument("--guide-weight", type=float)
    parser.add_argument("--retrieval-weight", type=float)
    parser.add_argument("--interior-positive-weight", type=float, default=1.0)
    parser.add_argument("--context-heads", type=int, choices=(1, 2, 4), default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--detail-head", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--detail-target", choices=("area", "nearest-exact"), default="area")
    parser.add_argument("--tar-variant", choices=("reparam", "plain", "diff", "spp", "spp1"), default="reparam")
    parser.add_argument("--decoder-variant", choices=("reparam", "plain", "none"), default="reparam")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.mode == "memory":
        if args.dataset_root is None or args.pretrained is None:
            parser.error("--mode memory needs --dataset-root and --pretrained")
        run_memory(args)
    else:
        run_flops_latency(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: models/test_CASA.py
================================================================================
"""Native pyramid, matching common initialization, task gradients and folding."""
import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from train_CASA import context_decay_names, training_losses, make_optimizer, verify_deployment


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    a,b=torch.randn(1,3,256,256),torch.randn(1,3,256,256)
    target=torch.randint(0,2,(1,1,256,256)).float()
    reference=None
    for variant in ('none','hyperbolic','qkv'):
        torch.manual_seed(2333)
        model=CASANet(module=variant).eval()
        count=sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
        with torch.no_grad():
            encoded=model.encoder(a)[1:]
            assert [tuple(x.shape[1:]) for x in encoded]==[(24,64,64),(32,32,32),(96,16,16),(320,8,8)]
            logits,aux=model(a,b,return_aux=True)
            outputs=model(a,b)
            assert len(logits)==len(outputs)==len(aux['guide_logits'])==4
            assert all(x.shape==target.shape for x in logits)
            assert all(torch.equal(z.sigmoid(),p) for z,p in zip(logits,outputs))
            assert torch.equal(model.predict(a,b),outputs[0])
            assert all(torch.isfinite(x).all() for x in model(a,a))
            if reference is None:
                reference=outputs[0]
            else:
                assert torch.equal(reference,outputs[0]), 'common initialization differs'
        model.train()
        optimizer=make_optimizer(model)
        context_ids={id(p) for p in model.context.parameters()}
        projection_ids={id(p) for n,p in model.named_parameters() if n in context_decay_names(model)}
        grouped=[id(p) for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group['params']]
        assert len(grouped)==len(set(grouped))==len(list(model.parameters()))
        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
            assert group['lr_scale']==1.
            assert all(group['weight_decay']==(0. if id(p) in context_ids and id(p) not in projection_ids
                                               else 1e-4) for p in group['params'])
        for _ in range(3):
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits,aux=model(a,b,return_aux=True)
            loss,main,guide=training_losses(logits,aux,target)
            assert torch.allclose(loss,main+.1*guide)
            loss.backward()
            assert all(torch.isfinite(p.grad).all() for p in model.parameters() if p.grad is not None)
            optimizer.step()
        assert model.context.guide.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
        if variant!='none':
            for layer in (model.context.qk,model.context.v,model.context.out,
                          model.context.local_dw,model.context.local_out):
                assert layer.weight.grad.abs().sum()>0
            assert (model.context.gamma_context.abs().sum(-1)>0).all()
            assert (model.context.gamma_detail.abs().sum(-1)>0).all()
        model.eval()
        _,report=verify_deployment(model,torch.cat((a,b),1))
        assert max(report['per_scale_max_diff'])<2e-4 and report['binary_disagreement']==0
        deployed=model.switch_to_deploy()
        print(variant,'train/deploy params',count,sum(p.numel() for p in deployed.parameters()),
              'guide/task gradients and folding PASS')


if __name__=='__main__':
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/test_CASA_resume.py
================================================================================
"""Optimizer/RNG continuation and deployment backend-state checks."""
import copy
import random

import numpy as np
import torch
from torch import nn

from train_CASA import (PROTOCOL, make_optimizer, restore_training_state,
                        rng_state, strict_fold_precision, update_learning_rate,
                        learning_rate, context_decay_names)


class SmallModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.module = "qkv"
        self.encoder = nn.Linear(3, 3)
        self.body = nn.Linear(3, 3)
        self.context = nn.ModuleDict({"qk": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "norm": nn.LayerNorm(3),
                                      "v": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "out": nn.Linear(3, 3, bias=False),
                                      "guide": nn.Linear(3, 3)})

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.context["norm"](self.encoder(x))
        return self.context["out"](self.context["qk"](self.body(x)) + self.context["v"](x)) + self.context["guide"](x)


def step(model, optimizer):
    x = torch.randn(4, 3) + random.random() + float(np.random.random())
    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    model(x).square().mean().backward()
    optimizer.step()


def backend_state():
    return (torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark, torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32,
            torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32, torch.get_float32_matmul_precision())


def check_decay_step():
    model = SmallModel().double()
    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
    with torch.no_grad():
        for parameter in model.parameters():
            parameter.fill_(1.0)
            parameter.grad = torch.zeros_like(parameter)
    optimizer.step()
    lr, decay, eps = 5e-4, 1e-4, 1e-8
    expected_context = 1 - lr * decay
    expected_body = 1 - lr * decay / (decay + eps)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.context["qk"].weight,
        torch.full_like(model.context["qk"].weight, expected_context), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.body.weight,
        torch.full_like(model.body.weight, expected_body), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    torch.testing.assert_close(model.encoder.weight,
        torch.full_like(model.encoder.weight, expected_body), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.equal(model.context["norm"].weight, torch.ones_like(model.context["norm"].weight))
    assert torch.equal(model.context["guide"].bias, torch.ones_like(model.context["guide"].bias))


def main():
    check_decay_step()
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    np.random.seed(2333)
    random.seed(2333)
    model = SmallModel()
    optimizer = make_optimizer(model)
    decay = {id(p): group["weight_decay"] for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    scales = {id(p): group["lr_scale"] for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    decay_names = context_decay_names(model)
    decoupled = {id(p): group["decoupled_weight_decay"]
                 for group in optimizer.param_groups for p in group["params"]}
    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
        expected_decay = 0.0 if name.startswith("context.") and name not in decay_names else 1e-4
        assert decay[id(parameter)] == expected_decay
        assert scales[id(parameter)] == 1.0
        assert decoupled[id(parameter)] == (name in decay_names)
        if name.startswith("context.norm.") or name == "context.guide.bias":
            assert decay[id(parameter)] == 0.0
        elif name.endswith(".weight"):
            assert decay[id(parameter)] == 1e-4
    for count in (0, 185, 199, 200, 20000, 39999):
        rate = learning_rate(5e-4, 1, count, 40000)
        update_learning_rate(optimizer, rate)
        assert optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"] == rate
        assert all(group["lr"] == rate for group in optimizer.param_groups[1:])
    for _ in range(4):
        step(model, optimizer)
    checkpoint = copy.deepcopy(dict(
        epoch=2, step=4, state_dict=model.state_dict(), optimizer=optimizer.state_dict(),
        manifest=dict(protocol=PROTOCOL, module="qkv", actual_steps=6, encoder_lr_mult=1.0), rng_state=rng_state()))
    for _ in range(2):
        step(model, optimizer)
    restored = SmallModel()
    restored_optimizer = make_optimizer(restored)
    epoch, count, exact_rng = restore_training_state(checkpoint, restored, restored_optimizer, 6, 2)
    assert (epoch, count, exact_rng) == (2, 4, True)
    for _ in range(2):
        step(restored, restored_optimizer)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(model.parameters(), restored.parameters()))
    assert all(int(state["step"]) == 6 for state in restored_optimizer.state.values())
    checkpoint.pop("rng_state")
    epoch, count, exact_rng = restore_training_state(checkpoint, restored, restored_optimizer, 6, 2)
    assert (epoch, count, exact_rng) == (2, 4, False)
    assert all(torch.equal(restored.state_dict()[key], value)
               for key, value in checkpoint["state_dict"].items())
    before = backend_state()
    try:
        with strict_fold_precision():
            assert backend_state() == (False, False, False, "highest")
            raise ValueError("exercise context exit")
    except ValueError:
        pass
    assert backend_state() == before
    print("Encoder LR, decoupled context/coupled body decay, optimizer/RNG continuation and backend restoration PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: models/test_ablation_variants.py
================================================================================
"""TAR and DCR ablation variants: parameter counts, folding and gradients."""
import copy

import torch
from torch import nn

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.str_dcr import DCRDecoder, PlainFuse1x1, PlainLocalBlock, SingleLevelDecoder, build_decoder
from model.str_tar import (DifferenceTARStage, DifferenceTemporal1x1, MultiScaleTAR,
                           PlainTARStage, PlainTemporal1x1)

PARAMS = {("reparam", "reparam"): (2047125, 1935810), ("reparam", "plain"): (2010250, 1935810),
          ("reparam", "none"): (1974730, 1900802), ("plain", "reparam"): (1974157, 1935810),
          ("plain", "plain"): (1937282, 1935810), ("plain", "none"): (1901762, 1900802),
          ("diff", "reparam"): (1943949, 1905602), ("diff", "plain"): (1907074, 1905602),
          ("diff", "none"): (1871554, 1870594)}


def build(tar, decoder, seed=2333):
    torch.manual_seed(seed)
    return CASANet(None, "none", 2333, 1, False, "area", tar, decoder)


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    image = torch.randn(2, 6, 256, 256)
    outputs_by_arm = {}
    for (tar, decoder), (expected, deployed) in PARAMS.items():
        model = build(tar, decoder).eval()
        assert sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()) == expected, (tar, decoder)
        folded = copy.deepcopy(model).eval().switch_to_deploy()
        assert sum(p.numel() for p in folded.parameters()) == deployed
        with torch.no_grad():
            outputs = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
            moved = folded(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
        assert len(outputs) == 4 and all(out.shape == (2, 1, 256, 256) for out in outputs)
        assert all(torch.allclose(a, b, atol=2e-4) for a, b in zip(outputs, moved))
        assert all(torch.isfinite(out).all() for out in outputs)
        outputs_by_arm[(tar, decoder)] = outputs
    # Removing a module must change behaviour (relative to the arm's own output scale),
    # and the graph itself must differ, otherwise the arm is a silent no-op.
    control = outputs_by_arm[("reparam", "reparam")]
    for arm in (("diff", "reparam"), ("reparam", "none"), ("diff", "none")):
        difference = max(float((a - b).abs().max()) for a, b in zip(control, outputs_by_arm[arm]))
        assert difference > 1e-4, arm
    diff_model = build("diff", "none")
    assert isinstance(diff_model.tar.stage1, DifferenceTARStage)
    assert isinstance(diff_model.tar.stage4, DifferenceTARStage)
    assert isinstance(diff_model.decoder, SingleLevelDecoder)
    assert not hasattr(diff_model.decoder, "fuse1") and not hasattr(diff_model.decoder, "refine")

    gradients = {("plain", "plain"): ["tar.stage1.temporal.proj.weight", "tar.stage4.temporal.proj.weight",
                                      "decoder.fuse1.fuse.weight", "decoder.refine.dw.weight"],
                 ("diff", "reparam"): ["tar.stage1.temporal.proj.weight", "tar.stage4.temporal.proj.weight",
                                       "decoder.fuse1.fuse_c.weight", "decoder.block1.dw.dw3.weight"],
                 ("reparam", "none"): ["tar.stage1.temporal.proj_c.weight", "decoder.proj.weight",
                                       "decoder.block.dw.weight"]}
    losses = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()
    for (tar, decoder), names in gradients.items():
        model = build(tar, decoder)
        logits, _ = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:], return_aux=True)
        losses(logits[0], (torch.rand(2, 1, 256, 256) > .5).float()).backward()
        parameters = dict(model.named_parameters())
        for name in names:
            assert parameters[name].grad is not None, name
            assert torch.isfinite(parameters[name].grad).all(), name
            assert parameters[name].grad.norm() > 0, name

    for factory in (lambda: MultiScaleTAR(variant="bogus"), lambda: DCRDecoder(variant="bogus"),
                    lambda: build_decoder(variant="bogus")):
        try:
            factory()
        except ValueError:
            pass
        else:
            raise AssertionError("unknown variant must be rejected")

    stage = PlainTARStage(24, 64)
    feature = torch.randn(1, 24, 32, 32)
    assert stage.block.dw.groups == 64 and isinstance(stage.temporal, PlainTemporal1x1)
    assert stage(feature, feature).shape == (1, 64, 32, 32)
    assert isinstance(DCRDecoder(variant="plain").fuse1, PlainFuse1x1)
    assert isinstance(DCRDecoder(variant="plain").refine, PlainLocalBlock)
    assert isinstance(DCRDecoder().refine, nn.Module) and DCRDecoder().variant == "reparam"

    difference_stage = DifferenceTARStage(24, 64)
    assert isinstance(difference_stage.temporal, DifferenceTemporal1x1)
    assert difference_stage(feature, -feature).shape == (1, 64, 32, 32)
    single = build_decoder(variant="none")
    assert isinstance(single, SingleLevelDecoder)
    pyramid = single.forward_pyramid([torch.randn(1, 64, 64, 64), torch.randn(1, 64, 32, 32),
                                      torch.randn(1, 64, 16, 16), torch.randn(1, 64, 8, 8)])
    assert [tuple(x.shape) for x in pyramid] == [(1, 64, 64, 64), (1, 64, 32, 32),
                                                 (1, 64, 16, 16), (1, 64, 8, 8)]
    print("TAR/DCR ablation variants: parameter counts, folding, gradients and no-op guards PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: models/test_attribution.py
================================================================================
"""Matched geometry, isolated losses and paired initialization."""
import torch
from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext,MatchedEuclideanContext
from train_CASA import auxiliary_weights,training_losses,guide_loss,loss_function


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4);torch.manual_seed(2333)
    for variant in ("none", "hyperbolic", "matched", "qkv"):
        assert auxiliary_weights(variant) == dict(guide=.1, geometric_retrieval=0.)
    h,e=HyperbolicContext(),MatchedEuclideanContext();e.load_state_dict(h.state_dict())
    x=torch.randn(1,64,8,8)
    qh,qe=h.project_queries(x),e.project_queries(x)
    assert torch.equal(qh,qe)
    assert sum(p.numel() for p in h.parameters())==sum(p.numel() for p in e.parameters())==11477
    assert not torch.allclose(h.squared_distance(qh.flatten(2,3),qh.flatten(2,3)),e.squared_distance(qe.flatten(2,3),qe.flatten(2,3)))
    target=torch.randint(0,2,(1,1,32,32)).float()
    outputs=tuple(torch.randn_like(target) for _ in range(4))
    guides=tuple(torch.randn(1,1,n,n,requires_grad=True) for n in (8,4,2,1))
    retrieval=tuple(torch.randn(1,1,n,n,requires_grad=True) for n in (8,4,2,1))
    aux=dict(guide_logits=guides,geometric_logits=retrieval)
    default, default_main, default_aux = training_losses(outputs, aux, target)
    torch.testing.assert_close(default, default_main + .1 * guide_loss(guides, target))
    unused = torch.autograd.grad(default, retrieval, allow_unused=True)
    assert all(value is None for value in unused)
    a,main,_=training_losses(outputs,aux,target,.1,.1)
    b,_,_=training_losses(outputs,aux,target,.1,0.)
    torch.testing.assert_close(a-b,.1*guide_loss(retrieval,target))
    torch.testing.assert_close(b,main+.1*guide_loss(guides,target))
    none_grad=torch.autograd.grad(b,retrieval,allow_unused=True)
    assert all(g is None or not g.any() for g in none_grad)
    op=HyperbolicContext()
    op.gamma_context.data.fill_(.2);op.gamma_detail.data.fill_(.2)
    value=torch.randn(1,64,1,1)
    constant=[value.expand(1,64,n,n).clone() for n in (16,8,4,4)]
    enhanced,_=op(constant)
    assert all(torch.allclose(a,b,atol=3e-6) for a,b in zip(enhanced,constant))
    out,_=op([torch.zeros_like(v) for v in constant])
    assert all(not z.any() for z in out)
    torch.manual_seed(11);m1=CASANet(module='hyperbolic',context_seed=22)
    torch.manual_seed(11);m2=CASANet(module='matched',context_seed=22)
    assert all(torch.equal(a,b) for a,b in zip(m1.state_dict().values(),m2.state_dict().values()))
    torch.manual_seed(11);m3=CASANet(module='hyperbolic',context_seed=23)
    assert torch.equal(m1.encoder.features[0][0].weight,m3.encoder.features[0][0].weight)
    assert not torch.equal(m1.context.qk.weight,m3.context.qk.weight)
    print('Matched H/E, shared content, isolated loss and paired initialization PASS')


if __name__=='__main__':main()
