# FILE: models/test_centered_readout.py
================================================================================
"""Centered context identity and differentiable content routing."""
import torch

from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext, MatchedEuclideanContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import project_tokens


def check(kind):
    op = kind().double()
    feature = torch.randn(1, 64, 4, 4, dtype=torch.float64)
    prototype = torch.randn(1, 224, 64, dtype=torch.float64)
    key, occupancy = op.project_keys(prototype)
    query = op.project_queries(feature).flatten(2, 3)
    values = project_tokens(op.v, prototype, op.heads)
    prior = op.guide(feature).sigmoid()
    probability = torch.randn(1, 1, 1, 224, dtype=torch.float64).softmax(-1).expand(1, 1, 16, 224)
    update, _, _, _, discrepancy, _ = op.readout(feature, probability, values, prior, occupancy)
    torch.testing.assert_close(update, torch.zeros_like(update), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    torch.testing.assert_close(discrepancy, torch.zeros_like(discrepancy), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    value_gradient = torch.autograd.grad((update * torch.randn_like(update)).sum(), values)[0]
    torch.testing.assert_close(value_gradient, torch.zeros_like(value_gradient), rtol=0, atol=1e-12)

    values = project_tokens(op.v, prototype, op.heads)
    probability = op.attention(query, key, torch.ones(1, 224, dtype=torch.float64), 0)
    update, *_ = op.readout(feature, probability, values, prior, occupancy)
    (update * torch.randn_like(update)).sum().backward()
    for name in ("qk", "v", "out", "gate"):
        gradient = getattr(op, name).weight.grad
        assert gradient is not None and torch.isfinite(gradient).all() and gradient.abs().sum() > 0, name
    assert torch.count_nonzero(op.gamma_context) == 0
    print(op.geometry + ": constant attention zero update/zero V gradient; varying attention QK/V/out/gate gradients PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    for kind in (HyperbolicContext, MatchedEuclideanContext):
        check(kind)

================================================================================
# FILE: models/test_context_geometry.py
================================================================================
"""Lorentz geometry checked against Poincare and Klein coordinate identities."""
import torch

from model.layers.lorentz_ops import (
    acosh1p_squared, lift, normalize_sum, pairwise_squared_distance,
)


def poincare_distance_reference(space_q, space_k):
    """FP64 reference using direct chords in the Poincare ball."""
    q, k = space_q.double(), space_k.double()
    time_q = torch.sqrt(1 + (q * q).sum(-1, keepdim=True))
    time_k = torch.sqrt(1 + (k * k).sum(-1, keepdim=True))
    ball_q, ball_k = q / (time_q + 1), k / (time_k + 1)
    chord = torch.cdist(ball_q, ball_k, compute_mode="donot_use_mm_for_euclid_dist")
    denominator = torch.sqrt(
        (1 - ball_q.square().sum(-1)).unsqueeze(-1)
        * (1 - ball_k.square().sum(-1)).unsqueeze(-2))
    return (2 * torch.asinh(chord / denominator)).square()


def klein_centroid_reference(points, weights):
    """Einstein barycenter in Klein coordinates, converted through the ball."""
    time, space = points[..., :1], points[..., 1:]
    klein = space / time
    weighted_mass = weights.unsqueeze(-1) * time.unsqueeze(-3)
    center = (weighted_mass * klein.unsqueeze(-3)).sum(-2) / weighted_mass.sum(-2)
    ball = center / (1 + torch.sqrt(1 - center.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)))
    denominator = 1 - ball.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    return torch.cat(((1 + ball.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)) / denominator,
                      2 * ball / denominator), dim=-1)


def lift_radius_and_gradient():
    directions = torch.randn(7, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    directions = directions / directions.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
    lengths = torch.tensor([0., .01, .25, 1., 3., 10., 30.],
                           dtype=torch.float64, requires_grad=True)
    point = lift(lengths[:, None] * directions)
    unit = point[:, 0].square() - point[:, 1:].square().sum(-1)
    torch.testing.assert_close(unit, torch.ones_like(unit), atol=5e-13, rtol=0)
    radius = torch.asinh(point[:, 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    torch.testing.assert_close(radius, torch.asinh(lengths), atol=2e-15, rtol=2e-15)
    gradient = torch.autograd.grad(radius[1:].sum(), lengths)[0][1:]
    expected = 1 / torch.sqrt(1 + lengths.detach()[1:].square())
    torch.testing.assert_close(gradient, expected, atol=1e-14, rtol=1e-13)
    assert (gradient > 0).all(), "large-radius spatial inputs lost radial gradients"
    assert radius.max() - radius.min() > 4
    print("Lorentz unit norm, variable radius and non-clipped radial gradients PASS")


def centroid_identities():
    space = torch.randn(2, 4, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * .7
    points = lift(space)
    weights = torch.rand(2, 4, 3, 7, dtype=torch.float64).softmax(-1)
    actual = normalize_sum(weights @ points)
    expected = klein_centroid_reference(points, weights)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    constant = points[..., :1, :].expand_as(points)
    actual = normalize_sum(weights @ constant)
    expected = points[..., :1, :].expand(-1, -1, 3, -1)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    selected = torch.nn.functional.one_hot(torch.tensor([1, 4, 6]), 7).double()
    actual = normalize_sum(selected @ points)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, points[..., [1, 4, 6], :], atol=2e-13, rtol=2e-13)

    opposite = torch.stack((space[..., 0, :], -space[..., 0, :]), dim=-2)
    center = normalize_sum(lift(opposite).mean(-2))
    origin = torch.zeros_like(center)
    origin[..., 0] = 1
    torch.testing.assert_close(center, origin, atol=1e-14, rtol=0)
    print("Lorentz centroids agree with Klein barycenters, constants and one-hot weights PASS")


def distance_references():
    q = torch.randn(2, 4, 11, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * 1.2
    k = torch.randn(2, 4, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64) * .8
    actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(q), lift(k))
    expected = poincare_distance_reference(q, k)
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-12, rtol=2e-12)

    radial_q = torch.tensor([0., .1, .8, 2., 4.], dtype=torch.float64)
    radial_k = torch.tensor([.03, .4, 1.3, 3.], dtype=torch.float64)
    a = torch.zeros(5, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    b = torch.zeros(4, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    a[:, 0], b[:, 0] = radial_q.sinh(), radial_k.sinh()
    expected = (radial_q[:, None] - radial_k[None, :]).square()
    actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b))
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=2e-11, rtol=2e-11)
    print("FP64 squared distances agree with Poincare chords and radial geodesics PASS")


def float32_distance_range():
    lengths = torch.tensor([0., 1e-4, .05, .5, 1., 3., 10., 30.])
    q = torch.randn(2, 4, len(lengths), 16)
    k = torch.randn_like(q)
    q = q / q.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * lengths[None, None, :, None]
    k = k / k.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * lengths[None, None, :, None]
    cases = [(q, k), (q, q), (q, q + torch.randn_like(q) * 1e-3)]
    maximum_error = 0.
    for left, right in cases:
        actual = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(left), lift(right))
        expected = poincare_distance_reference(left, right)
        maximum_error = max(maximum_error, float((actual.double() - expected).abs().max()))
        assert torch.isfinite(actual).all() and (actual >= 0).all()
        torch.testing.assert_close(actual.double(), expected, atol=2e-3, rtol=3e-5)
    print(f"FP32 spatial norm 0..30 squared-distance max absolute error={maximum_error:.3e} PASS")


def small_distance_limit():
    z = torch.cat((torch.tensor([0.], dtype=torch.float64),
                   torch.logspace(-14, -2, 25, dtype=torch.float64),
                   torch.tensor([1e-3 - 1e-9, 1e-3, 1e-3 + 1e-9], dtype=torch.float64)))
    z.requires_grad_()
    actual = acosh1p_squared(z)
    expected = 4 * torch.asinh(torch.sqrt(z.detach() / 2)).square()
    torch.testing.assert_close(actual, expected, atol=3e-15, rtol=2e-13)
    derivative = torch.autograd.grad(actual.sum(), z)[0]
    assert derivative[0] == 2 and torch.isfinite(derivative).all()
    positive_z = z.detach()[1:]
    expected_derivative = (4 * torch.asinh(torch.sqrt(positive_z / 2))
                           / torch.sqrt(positive_z * (positive_z + 2)))
    torch.testing.assert_close(derivative[1:], expected_derivative, atol=2e-12, rtol=2e-12)
    print("Squared acosh analytic value, derivative and branch-boundary limits PASS")


def distance_and_centroid_gradients():
    q = (torch.randn(1, 2, 3, dtype=torch.float64) * .6).requires_grad_()
    k = (torch.randn(1, 3, 3, dtype=torch.float64) * .6).requires_grad_()
    assert torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda a, b: pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b)),
                                    (q, k), eps=1e-6, atol=2e-6, rtol=2e-4)
    logits = torch.randn(1, 2, 3, dtype=torch.float64, requires_grad=True)
    assert torch.autograd.gradcheck(lambda points, scores: normalize_sum(scores.softmax(-1) @ lift(points)),
                                    (k, logits), eps=1e-6, atol=2e-6, rtol=2e-4)

    for radius in (0., .5, 3., 30.):
        source = torch.randn(1, 2, 5, 16)
        source = source / source.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True) * radius
        for perturbation in (0., 1e-6, .01):
            a = source.clone().requires_grad_()
            b = (source + perturbation * torch.randn_like(source)).requires_grad_()
            distance = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(a), lift(b))
            distance.sum().backward()
            assert torch.isfinite(a.grad).all() and torch.isfinite(b.grad).all()
            if radius == 0 and perturbation == 0:
                assert distance.count_nonzero() == 0
                assert a.grad.count_nonzero() == 0 and b.grad.count_nonzero() == 0
    print("Noncoincident distance/centroid gradcheck and FP32 coincident gradients PASS")


def euclidean_origin_limit():
    q = torch.randn(2, 3, 5, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    k = torch.randn(2, 3, 7, 16, dtype=torch.float64)
    euclidean = torch.cdist(q, k, compute_mode="donot_use_mm_for_euclid_dist").square()
    scale = 1e-4
    intrinsic = pairwise_squared_distance(lift(scale * q), lift(scale * k)) / scale ** 2
    torch.testing.assert_close(intrinsic, euclidean, atol=2e-5, rtol=2e-6)
    print("Lorentz spatial lift has the Euclidean metric at the origin PASS")


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(4701)
    lift_radius_and_gradient()
    centroid_identities()
    distance_references()
    float32_distance_range()
    small_distance_limit()
    distance_and_centroid_gradients()
    euclidean_origin_limit()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

================================================================================
# FILE: models/test_data_pipeline.py
================================================================================
"""RGB/date ordering, synchronized augmentation and stable binary loss checks."""
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import cv2
import numpy as np
import torch
from torch.nn import functional as F

from dataset import casa_transforms as transforms
from train_CASA import (MEAN, STD, learning_rate, loss_function, make_loader,
                        mask_boundary, training_losses, SpatialMetrics)


def main():
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
        root = Path(directory)
        for name in ("A", "B", "label", "list"):
            (root / name).mkdir()
        a = np.full((256, 256, 3), (17, 83, 211), dtype=np.uint8)
        b = np.full_like(a, (199, 67, 29))
        mask = np.zeros((256, 256), dtype=np.uint8)
        mask[100:180, 130:210] = 255
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "A" / "pair.png"), cv2.cvtColor(a, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "B" / "pair.png"), cv2.cvtColor(b, cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR))
        cv2.imwrite(str(root / "label" / "pair.png"), mask)
        (root / "list" / "test.txt").write_text("pair.png\n")
        image, target = next(iter(make_loader(root, "test", batch_size=1, workers=0)))
        expected = (torch.tensor([17, 83, 211, 199, 67, 29]) / 255 - torch.tensor(MEAN)) / torch.tensor(STD)
        assert torch.allclose(image[0, :, 0, 0], expected, atol=1e-6)
        assert torch.equal(target[0, 0], torch.from_numpy((mask >= 128).astype(np.int64)))

    mask = np.zeros((32, 32), dtype=np.uint8)
    mask[5:19, 12:25] = 255
    pair = np.stack((mask, mask, mask, 255-mask, 255-mask, 255-mask), -1)
    with patch.object(transforms.random, "random", return_value=0.0), \
            patch.object(transforms.random, "randrange", return_value=1):
        image, target = transforms.Compose([
            transforms.RandomFlip(), transforms.RandomRotate90(), transforms.RandomExchange()
        ])(pair, mask)
    assert np.array_equal(image[:, :, 0], 255-target)
    assert np.array_equal(image[:, :, 3], target)
    with patch.object(transforms.random, "random", return_value=0.0), \
            patch.object(transforms.random, "randint", side_effect=[1, 2, 3, 4]):
        cropped, crop_target = transforms.RandomCropResize(8)(pair, mask)
    assert np.array_equal(crop_target, cv2.resize(mask[1:-2, 3:-4], (32, 32), interpolation=cv2.INTER_NEAREST))
    assert np.array_equal(cropped, cv2.resize(pair[1:-2, 3:-4], (32, 32)))

    logits = torch.tensor([[[[-100.0, 100.0]]]], requires_grad=True)
    target = torch.tensor([[[[1.0, 0.0]]]])
    loss = loss_function((logits,), target)
    loss.backward()
    assert torch.isfinite(loss) and torch.isfinite(logits.grad).all()
    assert (logits.grad.abs() > 0.4).all()
    moderate = torch.tensor([[[[-2.0, 1.0]]]])
    probability = moderate.sigmoid()
    expected = F.binary_cross_entropy(probability, target) + 1 - (
        2 * (probability * target).sum() + 1e-5) / (probability.sum() + target.sum() + 1e-5)
    assert torch.allclose(loss_function((moderate,), target), expected)
    assert learning_rate(5e-4, 1, 185, 40145) == 5e-4 * .9 * 186 / 200 + 5e-5

    for value in (0.0, 1.0):
        constant = torch.full((1, 1, 16, 16), value)
        assert mask_boundary(constant).count_nonzero() == 0
    target = torch.zeros(1, 1, 16, 16)
    target[:, :, 4:12, 5:13] = 1
    edge = mask_boundary(target)
    assert edge[0, 0, 3:13, 4:14].sum() == 64
    assert edge[:, :, 0].sum() == 0 and edge[:, :, -1].sum() == 0
    assert F.adaptive_max_pool2d(edge, (4, 4)).sum() > 0
    aux = dict(guide_logits=tuple(torch.zeros(1, 1, side, side, requires_grad=True)
                                 for side in (8, 4, 2, 1)))
    outputs = tuple(torch.zeros_like(target, requires_grad=True) for _ in range(4))
    total, main_loss, guide = training_losses(outputs, aux, target)
    assert torch.equal(total, main_loss + .1 * guide)
    total.backward()
    assert all(z.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(z.grad).all() and z.grad.abs().sum() > 0
               for z in (*outputs, *aux["guide_logits"]))

    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(target > 0, target)
    scores = metric.scores()
    assert scores["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 1 and scores["boundary_2px"]["F1"] == 1
    assert scores["interior"]["recall"] == 1 and scores["interior"]["target_pixels"] == 16
    shifted = torch.roll(target, shifts=1, dims=-1)
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(shifted > 0, target)
    assert metric.scores()["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 1
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(torch.zeros_like(target).bool(), target)
    assert metric.scores()["boundary_1px"]["F1"] == 0
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["recall"] == 0
    small = torch.zeros_like(target)
    small[..., 8, 8] = 1
    metric = SpatialMetrics(torch.device("cpu"))
    metric.update(torch.zeros_like(small).bool(), small)
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["target_pixels"] == 0
    assert metric.scores()["interior"]["recall"] is None
    print("RGB/date order, synchronized augmentation, stable losses, boundary targets/metrics and warmup PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: models/test_direct_geometry.py
================================================================================
"""Direct projected geometry, class evidence and inference checks."""
import math

import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import project_tokens
from train_CASA import GUIDE_OBJECTIVE, guide_loss, training_losses, loss_function


def manifold_and_radius():
    op = HyperbolicContext().double()
    projected = torch.randn(2, 1, 7, 32, dtype=torch.double)
    point = op.geometric_point(projected)
    space = projected / math.sqrt(32)
    assert torch.equal(point[..., 1:], space)
    assert torch.allclose(point[..., 0].square() - point[..., 1:].square().sum(-1),
                          torch.ones_like(point[..., 0]), atol=1e-12)
    radius = torch.asinh(point[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    assert torch.allclose(radius, torch.asinh(projected.norm(dim=-1) / math.sqrt(32)), atol=1e-12)
    larger = torch.asinh(op.geometric_point(4 * projected)[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    assert (larger > radius).all()
    zeros = torch.zeros_like(projected)
    origin = op.geometric_point(zeros)
    assert torch.equal(origin[..., 0], torch.ones_like(origin[..., 0]))
    assert torch.equal(origin[..., 1:], zeros)
    for initial in (projected, zeros):
        query = initial.clone().requires_grad_()
        key = initial.clone().requires_grad_()
        diagonal = op.squared_distance(op.geometric_point(query), op.geometric_point(key)).diagonal(dim1=-2, dim2=-1)
        assert diagonal.abs().max() < 1e-12
        gradients = torch.autograd.grad(diagonal.sum(), (query, key))
        assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().max() < 1e-12 for g in gradients)
    print("Lorentz constraint, projected-radius sensitivity and finite zero-distance gradients PASS")


def content_and_guide_independence():
    op = HyperbolicContext().double()
    mean, delta = torch.randn(1, 3, 64, dtype=torch.double), torch.randn(1, 3, 64, dtype=torch.double)
    pooled_a = torch.stack((mean + delta, mean - delta), -2).mean(-2)
    pooled_b = torch.stack((mean + 5 * delta, mean - 5 * delta), -2).mean(-2)
    key_a, occupancy_a = op.project_keys(pooled_a)
    key_b, _ = op.project_keys(pooled_b)
    assert torch.allclose(key_a, key_b, atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.allclose(project_tokens(op.v, pooled_a, op.heads), project_tokens(op.v, pooled_b, op.heads), atol=1e-12)
    field = torch.randn(1, 64, 4, 4, dtype=torch.double)
    query_a = op.project_queries(field)
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.guide.weight.zero_()
        op.guide.bias.fill_(4.)
    query_b = op.project_queries(field)
    key_c, occupancy_c = op.project_keys(pooled_a)
    assert torch.equal(query_a, query_b) and torch.equal(key_a, key_c)
    assert not torch.equal(occupancy_a, occupancy_c)
    print("Shared pooled K/V content and Q/K coordinates independent of guide parameters PASS")


def evidence_and_fields():
    op = HyperbolicContext()
    assert op.tokens_per_scale == (128, 32, 32, 32)
    assert sum(p.numel() for p in op.parameters()) == 11477
    sizes = (16, 8, 4, 4)
    tar = [torch.randn(1, 64, n, n, requires_grad=True) for n in sizes]
    output, auxiliary = op(tar, return_aux=True)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(output, tar))
    target = torch.zeros(1, 1, 64, 64)
    target[..., 16:48, 16:48] = 1
    geometry_loss = guide_loss(auxiliary["geometric_logits"], target)
    gradients = torch.autograd.grad(geometry_loss, (op.qk.weight, *tar), retain_graph=True)
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in gradients)
    logit = torch.randn(1, 1, 6, 224)
    evidence = op.geometric_change_logit(logit, 2, 3)
    expected = logit[..., ::2].logsumexp(-1) - logit[..., 1::2].logsumexp(-1)
    assert torch.equal(evidence, expected.reshape(1, 1, 2, 3))
    predictions = tuple(torch.randn_like(target) for _ in range(4))
    loss, _, auxiliary_loss = training_losses(predictions, auxiliary, target)
    guide = guide_loss(auxiliary["guide_logits"], target)
    expected_auxiliary = guide
    assert torch.equal(auxiliary_loss, expected_auxiliary)
    assert torch.equal(loss, loss_function(predictions, target) + .1 * expected_auxiliary)
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.gamma_context.fill_(.1)
        op.gamma_detail.fill_(.1)
        constant = torch.randn(1, 64, 1, 1)
        fields = [constant.expand(1, 64, n, n).clone() for n in sizes]
        enhanced, _ = op(fields)
        assert all(torch.allclose(a, b, atol=3e-6) for a, b in zip(fields, enhanced))
        zeros = [torch.zeros_like(x) for x in fields]
        enhanced, _ = op(zeros)
        assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(zeros, enhanced))
    print("224 prototypes, geometric-evidence QK/TAR gradients, 0.1 guide budget, constant/zero fields PASS")


def inference_path():
    model = CASANet(module="hyperbolic").eval()
    def prohibited(*args):
        raise AssertionError("predict must not compute geometric auxiliary logits")
    model.context.geometric_change_logit = prohibited
    with torch.no_grad():
        result = model.predict(torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128), torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128))
        _, auxiliary = model(torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128), torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128), return_aux=True)
        assert "geometric_logits" not in auxiliary
    assert result.shape == (1, 1, 128, 128) and torch.isfinite(result).all()
    print("Prediction and evaluation exclude geometric auxiliary logsumexp PASS")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    manifold_and_radius()
    content_and_guide_independence()
    evidence_and_fields()
    inference_path()

# FILE: models/test_edge_supervision.py
================================================================================
"""Checks for the Sobel edge target and the two edge heads."""
import sys

import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

sys.path.insert(0, ".")

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.edge_supervision import EdgeHead, edge_loss, sobel_edge_map


def test_sobel_ring_and_flat():
    mask = torch.zeros(1, 1, 32, 32)
    mask[:, :, 8:24, 8:24] = 1.0
    edge = sobel_edge_map(mask, dilate=0)
    assert float(edge.max()) <= 1.0 + 1e-6
    assert abs(float(edge[0, 0, 16, 16])) < 1e-6, "interior must be flat"
    assert abs(float(edge[0, 0, 2, 2])) < 1e-6, "background must be flat"
    assert float(edge[0, 0, 8, 16]) > 0.9, "the boundary ring must be high"
    # a step of 1 pixel produces unit Sobel magnitude
    thin = torch.zeros(1, 1, 32, 32)
    thin[:, :, 16, :] = 1.0
    assert abs(float(sobel_edge_map(thin, 0).max()) - 1.0) < 1e-5


def test_dilation_widens_the_ring():
    mask = torch.zeros(1, 1, 32, 32)
    mask[:, :, 8:24, 8:24] = 1.0
    tight = sobel_edge_map(mask, dilate=0)
    wide = sobel_edge_map(mask, dilate=1)
    assert float(wide.sum()) > float(tight.sum())
    # the raw Sobel ring is already two pixels wide (the 3x3 kernel straddles the
    # step); dilation must extend it one pixel further on each side
    assert float(tight[0, 0, 7, 16]) > 0
    assert float(tight[0, 0, 6, 16]) == 0
    assert float(wide[0, 0, 6, 16]) > 0
    assert float(wide[0, 0, 5, 16]) == 0


def test_edge_target_ignores_the_crop_border():
    """A region that reaches the crop border must not be given a frame edge."""
    from train_CASA import mask_boundary

    full = torch.ones(1, 1, 256, 256)
    for dilate in (0, 1):
        edge = sobel_edge_map(full, dilate=dilate)
        assert int(edge.gt(0).sum()) == 0, f"full foreground, dilate={dilate}, is not all flat"

    # a band that touches the top, bottom and right borders has one real boundary
    mask = torch.zeros(1, 1, 256, 256)
    mask[:, :, :, 60:256] = 1.0
    edge = sobel_edge_map(mask, dilate=1)
    assert int(edge[..., :, -1].gt(0).sum()) == 0, "the right border must stay flat"
    # the real boundary at column 60 does reach the top and bottom borders, so
    # only the rest of those border lines has to stay flat
    keep = slice(54, 67)
    top = edge[..., 0, :].clone(); top[..., keep] = 0
    bottom = edge[..., -1, :].clone(); bottom[..., keep] = 0
    assert int(top.gt(0).sum()) == 0, "the top border must stay flat away from the boundary"
    assert int(bottom.gt(0).sum()) == 0, "the bottom border must stay flat away from the boundary"
    assert int(edge[..., :, 60].gt(0).sum()) > 0, "the real boundary must stay"

    # and the boundary metric used for evaluation agrees with it
    band = mask_boundary(mask, radius=1)
    assert int(band[..., :, -1].gt(0).sum()) == 0, "the metric band also ignores the right border"
    band_top = band[..., 0, :].clone(); band_top[..., keep] = 0
    assert int(band_top.gt(0).sum()) == 0, "the metric band also ignores the top border"
    assert int(band[..., :, 60].gt(0).sum()) > 0


def test_edge_loss_prefers_a_correct_prediction():
    mask = torch.zeros(1, 1, 32, 32)
    mask[:, :, 8:24, 8:24] = 1.0
    edge = sobel_edge_map(mask, 1)
    good = torch.logit(edge.clamp(1e-4, 1 - 1e-4))
    bad = torch.zeros_like(edge) - 3.0
    assert float(edge_loss(good, edge)) < float(edge_loss(bad, edge))


SIDE = 128  # the coarsest scale must stay at least as large as its 4x4 grid
# MobileNetV2 strides: the four TAR scales are side/4, side/8, side/16, side/32
EXPECTED = [(SIDE // 4, SIDE // 4), (SIDE // 8, SIDE // 8),
            (SIDE // 16, SIDE // 16), (SIDE // 32, SIDE // 32)]


def _edge_maps(module, tar_variant, edge_supervision):
    net = CASANet(module=module, tar_variant=tar_variant, edge_supervision=edge_supervision).train()
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, auxiliary = net(torch.randn(1, 3, SIDE, SIDE), torch.randn(1, 3, SIDE, SIDE),
                           return_aux=True)
    return net, auxiliary


def test_heads_are_opt_in():
    _, plain = _edge_maps("none", "cg", False)
    assert plain.get("edge_logits") is None
    _, armed = _edge_maps("none", "cg", True)
    maps = armed["edge_logits"]
    assert [tuple(m.shape[-2:]) for m in maps] == EXPECTED


def test_bridge_and_retrieval_both_expose_edges():
    _, bridge = _edge_maps("manifold", "cg", True)
    assert len(bridge["edge_logits"]) == 8, "four bridge maps plus four retrieval maps"
    _, retrieval_only = _edge_maps("manifold", "spp", True)
    assert len(retrieval_only["edge_logits"]) == 4
    _, difference = _edge_maps("difference", "spp", True)
    assert len(difference["edge_logits"]) == 4


def test_parameter_cost_is_sixty_five_per_head():
    head = EdgeHead(64)
    assert sum(p.numel() for p in head.parameters()) == 65
    base = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg").eval()
    armed = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg", edge_supervision=True).eval()
    delta = sum(p.numel() for p in armed.parameters()) - sum(p.numel() for p in base.parameters())
    assert delta == 130, delta


def test_gradients_reach_both_heads():
    net = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg", edge_supervision=True).train()
    a, b = torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE), torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE)
    target = torch.zeros(2, 1, SIDE, SIDE)
    target[:, :, SIDE // 4:3 * SIDE // 4, SIDE // 4:3 * SIDE // 4] = 1.0
    _, auxiliary = net(a, b, return_aux=True)
    edge = sobel_edge_map(target, 1)
    loss = sum(edge_loss(m, F.interpolate(edge, size=m.shape[-2:], mode="area"))
               for m in auxiliary["edge_logits"])
    loss.backward()
    assert net.tar.edge_head.proj.weight.grad is not None
    assert float(net.tar.edge_head.proj.weight.grad.abs().sum()) > 0
    assert net.context.edge_head.proj.weight.grad is not None
    assert float(net.context.edge_head.proj.weight.grad.abs().sum()) > 0


def test_retrieval_detach_keeps_the_head_but_cuts_the_trunk():
    """edge_detach="retrieval" keeps the head and the bridge, cuts the write-back."""
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    a, b = torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE), torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE)
    mask = torch.zeros(2, 1, SIDE, SIDE)
    mask[:, :, SIDE // 4:3 * SIDE // 4, SIDE // 5:4 * SIDE // 5] = 1.0
    target = sobel_edge_map(mask, dilate=1)

    def norms(detach):
        net = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg", edge_supervision=True,
                      edge_detach=detach).train()
        _, auxiliary = net(a, b, return_aux=True)
        sum(edge_loss(m, F.interpolate(target, size=m.shape[-2:], mode="area"))
            for m in auxiliary["edge_logits"]).backward()
        get = lambda name: (float(net.get_parameter(name).grad.norm())
                            if net.get_parameter(name).grad is not None else 0.0)
        return (get("tar.edge_head.proj.weight"), get("context.edge_head.proj.weight"),
                get("context.qk.weight"))

    tar_full, head_full, trunk_full = norms("none")
    tar_cut, head_cut, trunk_cut = norms("retrieval")
    assert tar_full > 0 and tar_cut > 0, (tar_full, tar_cut)
    assert head_full > 0 and head_cut > 0, (head_full, head_cut)
    assert trunk_full > 0, trunk_full
    assert trunk_cut == 0.0, trunk_cut
    print(f"[EDGE-DETACH] full tar={tar_full:.2e} trunk={trunk_full:.2e}; "
          f"cut tar={tar_cut:.2e} head={head_cut:.2e} trunk={trunk_cut:.2e}")


def test_retrieval_detach_is_the_default():
    """Run6: leaving the tap attached cost 1.09 pp on SYSU, so it is cut by default."""
    net = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg", edge_supervision=True)
    assert net.context.edge_detach == "retrieval"
    a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, SIDE, SIDE), torch.randn(1, 3, SIDE, SIDE)
    net.train()
    net.zero_grad()
    _, auxiliary = net(a, b, return_aux=True)
    maps = auxiliary["edge_logits"]
    loss = torch.stack([edge_loss(m, torch.zeros_like(m)) for m in maps]).mean()
    loss.backward()
    qk_grad = net.context.qk.weight.grad
    assert qk_grad is None or float(qk_grad.abs().sum()) == 0
    assert net.context.edge_head.proj.weight.grad.abs().sum() > 0


def test_detach_does_not_touch_the_segmentation_gradient():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    a, b = torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE), torch.randn(2, 3, SIDE, SIDE)
    mask = torch.zeros(2, 1, SIDE, SIDE)
    mask[:, :, SIDE // 4:3 * SIDE // 4, SIDE // 5:4 * SIDE // 5] = 1.0
    for detach in ("none", "retrieval"):
        net = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg", edge_supervision=True,
                      edge_detach=detach).train()
        with torch.no_grad():
            net.context.gamma_context.fill_(0.5)
        outputs = net(a, b)
        sum(F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            torch.logit(v.clamp(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)),
            F.interpolate(mask, size=v.shape[-2:])) for v in outputs).backward()
        grad = net.get_parameter("context.qk.weight").grad
        assert grad is not None and float(grad.norm()) > 0, detach
    print("[EDGE-DETACH] segmentation still reaches the retrieval in both modes")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    for name, fn in list(globals().items()):
        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn):
            fn()
            print(f"[PASS] {name}")

# FILE: models/test_hyperbolic_context.py
================================================================================
"""Cross-scale source and parameter gradients of the centered context."""
import torch

from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext, class_assignments
from model.layers.pyramid_context import pool_features, project_tokens


def compression_and_geometry():
    op = HyperbolicContext().double()
    p = torch.full((1, 1, 8, 8), .5, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    x = torch.randn(1, 64, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    weights, support = class_assignments(p, 1)
    assert weights.shape == (1, 1, 2, 64)
    assert torch.allclose(weights.sum(-1), torch.ones_like(weights[..., 0]))
    assert torch.allclose(support, torch.full_like(support, .5))
    prototype = pool_features(x, weights, 1)
    loss = prototype[:, 0].square().mean()
    gradients = torch.autograd.grad(loss, (x, p))
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in gradients)
    sparse = torch.full_like(p, .05)
    sparse[..., 0, 0] = .6
    sparse_weights, _ = class_assignments(sparse, 1)
    assert .15 < sparse_weights[0, 0, 0, 0] < .17
    q = torch.randn(1, op.heads, 12, op.head_dim, dtype=torch.double)
    points = op.geometric_point(q)
    radius = torch.asinh(points[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
    assert torch.allclose(radius, torch.asinh(q.norm(dim=-1) / op.head_dim ** .5), atol=1e-12)
    assert (torch.asinh(op.geometric_point(3 * q)[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1)) > radius).all()
    assert torch.allclose(points[..., 0].square() - points[..., 1:].square().sum(-1), torch.ones_like(radius), atol=2e-14)
    # K and V depend on the same P, without a separate region-scatter statistic.
    mean = torch.randn(1, 1, 64, dtype=torch.double)
    delta = torch.randn_like(mean)
    two = torch.cat((mean + delta, mean - delta), 1).mean(1, keepdim=True)
    large = torch.cat((mean + 5 * delta, mean - 5 * delta), 1).mean(1, keepdim=True)
    assert torch.allclose(op.project_keys(two)[0], op.project_keys(large)[0], atol=1e-12)
    assert torch.allclose(project_tokens(op.v, two, op.heads), project_tokens(op.v, large, op.heads), atol=1e-12)
    print('Projected radius, scatter invariance, sparse targets, class prototypes and gradients PASS')


def module():
    op = HyperbolicContext()
    sizes = (32, 16, 8, 4)
    tar = [torch.randn(1, 64, n, n, requires_grad=True) for n in sizes]
    output, _ = op(tar)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(tar, output))
    with torch.no_grad():
        op.gamma_context.fill_(.1)
        op.gamma_detail.fill_(.1)
    constant = torch.randn(1, 64, 1, 1)
    fields = [constant.expand(1, 64, n, n).clone() for n in sizes]
    output, _ = op(fields)
    assert all(torch.allclose(a, b, atol=3e-6) for a, b in zip(fields, output))
    zero = [torch.zeros_like(x) for x in tar]
    output, _ = op(zero)
    assert all(torch.equal(a, b) for a, b in zip(zero, output))
    op.collect_diag = True
    output, _ = op(tar)
    grads = torch.autograd.grad(output[0].square().mean(), tar, retain_graph=True)
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads)
    sum(o.square().mean() for o in output).backward()
    for name, parameter in op.named_parameters():
        assert parameter.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all(), name
        assert parameter.grad.abs().sum() > 0, name
    for index, stage in enumerate(op.last_diag.values()):
        assert stage['contexts'] == 224 and stage['finite']
        assert 0 < stage['gate_mean'] < 1
        assert stage['adoption_source'] == 'learned_value_correction_gate'
    print('Identity, constant/zero fields, all parameter and cross-scale gradients PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    compression_and_geometry()
    module()

# FILE: models/test_interior_loss.py
================================================================================
"""Interior weighting changes only the first BCE and its positive interior gradient."""
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from train_CASA import foreground_interior_bce, loss_function, mask_boundary


def main():
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    y = torch.zeros(1, 1, 16, 16, dtype=torch.double)
    y[:, :, 3:13, 3:13] = 1
    z = torch.randn_like(y, requires_grad=True)
    raw = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(z, y)
    assert torch.equal(raw, foreground_interior_bce(z, y, 1.))
    inner = y * (mask_boundary(y, 2) == 0)
    weight = 1 + .25 * inner
    got = foreground_interior_bce(z, y, 1.25)
    expected = (weight * F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(z, y, reduction='none')).sum() / weight.sum()
    torch.testing.assert_close(got, expected, atol=0, rtol=0)
    a, = torch.autograd.grad(raw, z, retain_graph=True)
    b, = torch.autograd.grad(got, z)
    torch.testing.assert_close(b, a * weight * y.numel() / weight.sum())
    for target in [torch.zeros_like(y), (torch.arange(16)[None, None, :, None] == 8).expand_as(y).double()]:
        torch.testing.assert_close(foreground_interior_bce(z, target, 1.25),
                                   F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(z, target), atol=0, rtol=0)
    outputs = [torch.randn_like(y, requires_grad=True) for _ in range(4)]
    base = loss_function(outputs, y)
    weighted = loss_function(outputs, y, 1.25)
    expected_delta = foreground_interior_bce(outputs[0], y, 1.25) - foreground_interior_bce(outputs[0], y)
    torch.testing.assert_close(weighted - base, expected_delta)
    ga = torch.autograd.grad(base, outputs, retain_graph=True)
    gb = torch.autograd.grad(weighted, outputs)
    for i in (1, 2, 3):
        torch.testing.assert_close(ga[i], gb[i], atol=0, rtol=0)
    print('Interior BCE normalization, target band, gradients and first-head-only scope PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

# FILE: models/test_minor_goal.py
================================================================================
"""Tests for the minor-goal arms: multi-scale difference pooling and difference retrieval."""
import copy
import unittest

import torch

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.str_tar import MultiScaleTAR, RepSPPBlock, SPPTARStage, embed_depthwise_kernel
from model.layers.difference_context import DifferenceContext
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext


def count(model):
    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())


class RepSPPBlockTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_fold_matches_training_path(self):
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        block = RepSPPBlock(16).eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            block.bn_branches[1].weight.fill_(0.7)
            block.bn_branches[2].weight.fill_(0.4)
            block.bn_branches[3].weight.fill_(0.2)
        x = torch.randn(2, 16, 32, 32)
        with torch.no_grad():
            reference = block(x)
        folded = copy.deepcopy(block).switch_to_deploy()
        with torch.no_grad():
            result = folded(x)
        self.assertLess(float((reference - result).abs().max()), 2e-4)

    def test_kernel_embedding_keeps_the_weight_device(self):
        # A meta tensor exposes the device bug that only shows up on CUDA: the
        # padding buffer must be created on the kernel's own device.
        weight = torch.zeros(4, 1, 3, 3, device="meta")
        embedded = embed_depthwise_kernel(weight, 7)
        self.assertEqual(embedded.device, weight.device)
        self.assertEqual(tuple(embedded.shape), (4, 1, 7, 7))

    def test_summed_branches_are_centred(self):
        torch.manual_seed(1)
        block = RepSPPBlock(8)
        weight, bias = block.get_equivalent_kernel()
        target = block.max_kernel
        expected = torch.zeros_like(weight)
        expected_bias = torch.zeros_like(bias)
        for conv, bn in zip(block.dw_branches, block.bn_branches):
            k = conv.weight.shape[-1]
            offset = (target - k) // 2
            scale = (bn.weight.detach().double()
                     / torch.sqrt(bn.running_var.detach().double() + bn.eps))
            branch = conv.weight.detach().double() * scale.view(-1, 1, 1, 1)
            expected[..., offset:offset + k, offset:offset + k] += branch
            expected_bias += (bn.bias.detach().double()
                              - bn.running_mean.detach().double() * scale)
        self.assertLess(float((weight - expected).abs().max()), 1e-10)
        self.assertLess(float((bias - expected_bias).abs().max()), 1e-10)

        # Everything outside the embedded kernels stays exactly zero.
        mask = torch.zeros(target, target, dtype=torch.float64)
        for k in block.kernels:
            offset = (target - k) // 2
            mask[offset:offset + k, offset:offset + k] = 1
        self.assertEqual(float((weight * (1 - mask)).abs().max()), 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(tuple(bias.shape), (8,))

    def test_residual_and_identity_initialisation(self):
        block = RepSPPBlock(8)
        x = torch.randn(1, 8, 8, 8)
        with torch.no_grad():
            y = block(x)
        # Wider branches start switched off, so only the 1x1 identity path is active.
        self.assertEqual(float(block.bn_branches[1].weight.abs().sum()), 0.0)
        self.assertTrue(torch.isfinite(y).all())


class SPPStageTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_returns_fused_and_difference_streams(self):
        stage = SPPTARStage(24, 64)
        pre, post = torch.randn(2, 24, 64, 64), torch.randn(2, 24, 64, 64)
        fused, difference = stage(pre, post)
        self.assertEqual(tuple(fused.shape), (2, 64, 64, 64))
        self.assertEqual(tuple(difference.shape), (2, 64, 64, 64))
        with torch.no_grad():
            identical = stage(pre, pre)[1]
        self.assertEqual(float(identical.abs().max()), 0.0)

    def test_tar_variant_returns_difference(self):
        tar = MultiScaleTAR((24, 32, 96, 320), dim=64, variant="spp", return_difference=True)
        pre = [torch.randn(1, c, s, s) for c, s in ((24, 64), (32, 32), (96, 16), (320, 8))]
        post = [torch.randn(1, c, s, s) for c, s in ((24, 64), (32, 32), (96, 16), (320, 8))]
        fused, difference = tar(pre, post)
        self.assertEqual([tuple(x.shape) for x in fused],
                         [(1, 64, 64, 64), (1, 64, 32, 32), (1, 64, 16, 16), (1, 64, 8, 8)])
        self.assertEqual([tuple(x.shape) for x in difference], [tuple(x.shape) for x in fused])
        self.assertEqual(MultiScaleTAR((24, 32, 96, 320), dim=64, variant="spp")(pre, post)[0].shape[1], 64)


class DifferenceContextTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_requires_difference_stream(self):
        context = DifferenceContext()
        tar = [torch.zeros(1, 64, 64, 64), torch.zeros(1, 64, 32, 32),
               torch.zeros(1, 64, 16, 16), torch.zeros(1, 64, 8, 8)]
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            context(tar)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            context(tar, difference=tar[:2])

    def test_keys_follow_the_difference_not_the_feature(self):
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        context = DifferenceContext().eval()
        tar = [torch.randn(1, 64, s, s) for s in (64, 32, 16, 8)]
        difference = [torch.randn(1, 64, s, s) for s in (64, 32, 16, 8)]
        captured = {}
        original = context.prototype_features

        def spy(normalized, difference=None):
            features = original(normalized, difference)
            captured["features"] = features
            return features

        context.prototype_features = spy
        with torch.no_grad():
            context(tar, difference)
        reference = captured["features"]
        with torch.no_grad():
            context(tar, [d.roll(shifts=1, dims=-1) for d in difference])
        changed = captured["features"]
        self.assertFalse(torch.allclose(reference[0], changed[0], atol=1e-6))
        # Layer normalisation makes the prototypes scale invariant; only the
        # spatial content of the difference may change them.
        with torch.no_grad():
            context(tar, [d * 3.0 for d in difference])
        self.assertTrue(torch.allclose(reference[0], captured["features"][0], atol=1e-5))

    def test_inherits_geometry_and_readout(self):
        context = DifferenceContext()
        self.assertIsInstance(context, HyperbolicContext)
        self.assertEqual(context.tokens_per_scale, (128, 32, 32, 32))
        self.assertEqual(context.geometry, "hyperbolic_difference")
        self.assertEqual(count(context), count(HyperbolicContext()) + 2 * 64)


class NetworkTest(unittest.TestCase):
    ARMS = {
        ("none", "reparam"): (2047125, 1935810),
        ("none", "spp"): (2118293, 1995714),
        ("difference", "spp"): (2129705, 2007126),
    }

    def test_parameter_counts_and_deploy_fidelity(self):
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128), torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128)
        for (module, tar_variant), (trainable, deployed) in self.ARMS.items():
            model = CASANet(None, module=module, tar_variant=tar_variant).eval()
            self.assertEqual(count(model), trainable, (module, tar_variant))
            folded = copy.deepcopy(model).switch_to_deploy()
            self.assertEqual(count(folded), deployed, (module, tar_variant))
            with torch.no_grad():
                reference, deployed_output = model(a, b), folded(a, b)
            self.assertLess(max(float((x - y).abs().max()) for x, y in zip(reference, deployed_output)),
                            2e-4)

    def test_difference_requires_spp(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            CASANet(None, module="difference", tar_variant="reparam")

    def test_difference_context_carries_gradient_after_gate_opens(self):
        torch.manual_seed(0)
        model = CASANet(None, module="difference", tar_variant="spp").train()
        a, b = torch.randn(2, 3, 128, 128), torch.randn(2, 3, 128, 128)
        target = (torch.rand(2, 1, 128, 128) > 0.9).float()
        outputs = model(a, b)
        sum(torch.nn.functional.binary_cross_entropy(x, target) for x in outputs).backward()
        self.assertGreater(float(model.context.gamma_context.grad.norm()), 0.0)
        model.zero_grad()
        with torch.no_grad():
            model.context.gamma_context.fill_(0.5)
        outputs = model(a, b)
        sum(torch.nn.functional.binary_cross_entropy(x, target) for x in outputs).backward()
        for name in ("qk.weight", "v.weight", "out.weight", "norm_diff.weight"):
            grad = dict(model.context.named_parameters())[name].grad
            self.assertIsNotNone(grad, name)
            self.assertGreater(float(grad.norm()), 0.0, name)
        self.assertGreater(float(model.tar.stage1.spp.bn_branches[3].weight.grad.norm()), 0.0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

def test_spp1_keeps_one_branch():
    block = RepSPPBlock(64, kernels=(1,))
    assert block.max_kernel == 1
    assert len(block.dw_branches) == 1
    x = torch.randn(1, 64, 32, 32)
    block.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        out = block(x)
    assert torch.isfinite(out).all()
    # A single 1x1 branch must fold to the identity-wide pathway only.
    weight, bias = block.get_equivalent_kernel()
    assert weight.shape == (64, 1, 1, 1)


def test_spp1_arm_is_cheaper_than_spp():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    spp = CASANet(module="none", tar_variant="spp")
    spp1 = CASANet(module="none", tar_variant="spp1")
    count = lambda m: sum(p.numel() for p in m.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
    assert count(spp1) < count(spp)
    assert count(spp1) == 2095509


def test_difference_context_accepts_plain_difference_stream():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    model = CASANet(module="difference", tar_variant="diff")
    model.eval()
    a, b = torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128), torch.randn(1, 3, 128, 128)
    with torch.no_grad():
        outputs = model(a, b)
    assert torch.isfinite(outputs[0]).all()
    total = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad)
    assert total == 1955361


def test_difference_context_still_requires_a_difference_stream():
    try:
        CASANet(module="difference", tar_variant="reparam")
    except ValueError as error:
        assert "temporal difference stream" in str(error)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("the difference context accepted a fused-only TAR")

def test_nested_aggregation_matches_the_2x2_parent_mean():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    context = HyperbolicContext(channels=8, kv_hierarchy="nested")
    assert context.tokens_per_scale == (128, 32, 8, 2), context.tokens_per_scale
    prototypes = [torch.randn(1, 2 * grid ** 2, 8) for grid in (8, 4, 2, 1)]
    aggregated = context.aggregate_nested(prototypes)
    assert len(aggregated) == 4
    # child at scale 1 was built from the scale-0 grid, so check one token by hand
    parent = prototypes[0].reshape(1, 8, 8, 2, 8)
    expected = parent[:, 0:2, 0:2, 0, :].mean(dim=(1, 2)).reshape(1, 1, 8)
    actual = aggregated[1].reshape(1, 4, 4, 2, 8)[:, 0, 0, 0, :].reshape(1, 1, 8)
    assert 0.5 * (prototypes[1].reshape(1, 4, 4, 2, 8)[:, 0, 0, 0, :].reshape(1, 1, 8)
                  + expected).allclose(actual, atol=1e-6)


def test_support_cell_is_the_mass_alias():
    cell = HyperbolicContext(channels=8, support_mode="cell")
    mass = HyperbolicContext(channels=8, support_mode="mass")
    assert cell.support_prior == mass.support_prior == "mass"
    assert cell.tokens_per_scale == mass.tokens_per_scale
    assert count(cell) == count(mass)
    for mode in ("pixel", "none"):
        assert HyperbolicContext(channels=8, support_mode=mode).support_prior == mode
    try:
        HyperbolicContext(channels=8, support_mode="bogus")
    except ValueError as error:
        assert "unknown support_mode" in str(error)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("an unknown support mode was accepted")

# FILE: models/test_pyramid_context.py

"""Compression fidelity, constant fields and cross-scale task gradients."""
import torch

from model.layers.euclidean_context import EuclideanContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import assignments, pool_features, highpass, regions


def compression_semantics():
    feature = torch.randn(1, 64, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double, requires_grad=True)
    probability = torch.zeros(1, 1, 8, 8, dtype=torch.double)
    probability[..., 0, 0] = 1
    weights, confidence = assignments(probability, 1)
    prototype = pool_features(feature, weights, 1)
    assert weights[0, 0, 0, 0] > .9999
    assert torch.allclose(prototype[:, 0], feature[..., 0, 0], atol=3e-4)
    assert torch.allclose(confidence, torch.ones_like(confidence), atol=1e-9, rtol=0), "sparse confident target lost prior mass"
    for value in (0., .01, .5, .99, 1.):
        weights, confidence = assignments(torch.full_like(probability, value), 1)
        assert torch.allclose(weights.sum(-1), torch.ones_like(weights[..., 0]))
        prototype = pool_features(feature, weights, 1)
        assert torch.allclose(prototype[:, 0], prototype[:, 1], atol=1e-12)
        expected = torch.tensor([[max(value**2, 1e-6), max((1-value)**2, 1e-6)]], dtype=torch.double)
        assert torch.allclose(confidence, expected, atol=1e-12)
    logits = torch.randn_like(probability, requires_grad=True)
    weights, _ = assignments(logits.sigmoid(), 2)
    pooled = pool_features(feature, weights, 2)
    grads = torch.autograd.grad(pooled.square().sum(), (feature, logits))
    assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in grads)
    sparse = torch.full_like(probability,.05)
    sparse[...,0,0]=.6
    sharpened,_=assignments(sparse,1)
    assert .69 < sharpened[0,0,0,0] < .70
    operator = EuclideanContext().double()
    dense = operator.project_queries(feature)
    expected = torch.einsum("brtp,bhrpd->bhrtd", weights, regions(dense, 2)).flatten(2, 3)
    actual = operator.pool_keys(dense, pooled, weights, 2)
    assert torch.allclose(actual, expected, atol=1e-12), "pool/linear equivalence failed"
    print("sparse targets, unsupported classes, assignment gradients and pool/linear equivalence PASS")


def contexts():
    sizes = (32,16,8,4)
    for context_type in (EuclideanContext,):
        op = context_type()
        tar = [torch.randn(1,64,n,n,requires_grad=True) for n in sizes]
        output, aux = op(tar)
        assert all(torch.equal(x,y) for x,y in zip(output,tar))
        assert len(aux['guide_logits']) == 4
        with torch.no_grad():
            op.gamma_context.fill_(.1)
            op.gamma_detail.fill_(.1)
        constant = torch.randn(1,64,1,1)
        fields = [constant.expand(1,64,n,n).clone() for n in sizes]
        output, _ = op(fields)
        assert all(torch.allclose(x,y,atol=3e-6,rtol=1e-6) for x,y in zip(output,fields)), "constant field changed"
        assert highpass(torch.ones(1,64,8,8)).abs().max() == 0
        op.collect_diag = True
        output, _ = op(tar)
        gradients = torch.autograd.grad(output[0].square().mean(), tar, retain_graph=True)
        assert all(torch.isfinite(g).all() and g.abs().sum() > 0 for g in gradients), "cross-scale gradient missing"
        sum(x.square().mean() for x in output).backward()
        for name, parameter in op.named_parameters():
            assert parameter.grad is not None and torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all(), name
            assert parameter.grad.abs().sum() > 0, name
        for i in range(4):
            diag=op.last_diag[f'stage{i+1}']
            assert diag['finite'] and diag['contexts']==224
            assert diag['context_residual_to_feature_rms']>0
            if i<2:
                assert diag['local_residual_to_feature_rms']>0
        # Identical distances and class confidence should not prefer the larger source grid.
        query=op.project_queries(torch.zeros(1,64,1,3)).flatten(2,3)
        key=op.project_queries(torch.zeros(1,64,1,224)).flatten(2,3)
        attention=op.attention(query,key,torch.ones(1,224),0)
        mass=torch.stack([p.sum(-1) for p in attention.split(op.tokens_per_scale,-1)],-1)
        assert torch.allclose(mass,torch.full_like(mass,.2),atol=1e-6)
        # With no matching prototype, the self candidate receives nearly all mass.
        remote=op.project_queries(torch.full((1,64,1,224),100.)).flatten(2,3)
        remote_attention=op.attention(query,remote,torch.ones(1,224),0)
        assert remote_attention.sum(-1).max()<1e-4
        # Uniformly ambiguous pairs have half the total prior of confident pairs.
        uncertain=op.attention(query,key,torch.full((1,224),.25),0).sum(-1)
        confident=op.attention(query,key,torch.tensor([1.,1e-6]).repeat(112)[None],0).sum(-1)
        assert torch.all(uncertain < confident)
        print(context_type.__name__, 'identity, constant preservation, all parameter/cross-scale gradients PASS')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    torch.set_num_threads(4)
    torch.manual_seed(2333)
    compression_semantics()
    contexts()

# FILE: models/test_run4_modules.py

"""Checks for the Run4 modules: change-gated TAR and manifold retrieval."""
import copy
import sys

import torch
from torch import nn

sys.path.insert(0, "models")

from model.casa_net import CASANet
from model.str_tar import ChangeGateTARStage, MultiScaleTAR
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import FLAT_GRIDS, HyperbolicContext
from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift, minkowski_barycenter
from model.layers.manifold_context import ManifoldContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import channel_norm


def test_contrast_normalisation_ignores_exposure():
    stage = ChangeGateTARStage(8, 16).eval()
    P = torch.rand(1, 8, 16, 16)
    Q = torch.rand(1, 8, 16, 16)
    _, d_ref = stage(P, Q)
    _, d_scaled = stage(P * 1.0, Q * 3.0)
    # interior pixels normalise identically; borders share the padded window
    rel = (d_ref[:, :, 4:-4, 4:-4] - d_scaled[:, :, 4:-4, 4:-4]).abs().max()
    assert rel < 1e-3, rel


def test_signed_difference_is_asymmetric_and_zero_on_identity():
    stage = ChangeGateTARStage(8, 16).eval()
    P = torch.rand(1, 8, 16, 16)
    Q = torch.rand(1, 8, 16, 16) * 0.5
    _, forward_delta = stage(P, Q)
    _, backward_delta = stage(Q, P)
    assert forward_delta.abs().max() > 1e-4
    assert (forward_delta - backward_delta).abs().max() > 1e-4
    _, same = stage(P, P.clone())
    assert same.abs().max() == 0


def test_cross_scale_evidence_propagates_downward():
    tar = MultiScaleTAR((24, 32, 96, 320), 64, variant="cg", return_difference=True).eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        tar.stage1.difference.weight.zero_()
        tar.stage1.bn_difference.weight.zero_()
        tar.stage1.bn_difference.bias.zero_()
        tar.stage1.beta.zero_()
    P = [torch.rand(1, c, s, s) for c, s in ((24, 64), (32, 32), (96, 16), (320, 8))]
    Q = [torch.rand(1, c, s, s) for c, s in ((24, 64), (32, 32), (96, 16), (320, 8))]
    with torch.no_grad():
        _, deltas = tar(P, Q)
    expected = torch.nn.functional.interpolate(
        deltas[1], size=deltas[0].shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
    # stage-1 delta is zeroed, so d1 must equal the upsampled, already-propagated d2
    assert torch.allclose(deltas[0], expected, atol=1e-5)
    assert [tuple(d.shape[-2:]) for d in deltas] == [(64, 64), (32, 32), (16, 16), (8, 8)]


def test_barycenter_is_idempotent_and_stays_on_the_manifold():
    tokens, dim = 5, 8
    value = torch.randn(2, 1, 3, tokens, dim)          # three queries per example
    values = value[:, :, :, :1].expand(-1, -1, -1, tokens, -1)   # all tokens identical
    weights = torch.softmax(torch.randn(2, 1, 3, tokens), dim=-1).unsqueeze(-1)
    out = minkowski_barycenter(values, weights)
    assert torch.allclose(out, value[:, :, :, 0], atol=1e-6)
    distinct = torch.randn(2, 1, 3, tokens, dim)
    uniform = torch.full((2, 1, 3, tokens, 1), 1 / tokens)
    mixed = minkowski_barycenter(distinct, uniform)
    euclidean = (distinct * (1 / tokens)).sum(-2)
    assert (mixed - euclidean).abs().max() > 1e-3
    lifted = lift(mixed)
    norm = lifted[..., :1].square() - lifted[..., 1:].square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    assert torch.allclose(norm, torch.ones_like(norm), atol=1e-5)


def test_manifold_context_shares_geometry_but_not_the_readout():
    base = HyperbolicContext(grid_sizes=FLAT_GRIDS)
    manifold = ManifoldContext(grid_sizes=FLAT_GRIDS)
    base.scale_log_temperature = None if False else None
    with torch.no_grad():
        manifold.scale_log_temperature.copy_(torch.tensor([0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9]))
    tau_base = base.scale_temperature(2)
    tau_scaled = manifold.scale_temperature(2)
    assert (tau_scaled - tau_base).abs().max() > 1e-3
    assert (manifold.scale_temperature(0) - manifold.scale_temperature(3)).abs().max() > 1e-3
    assert torch.allclose(base.scale_temperature(0), base.scale_temperature(3))
    # the independent prior must not move when the guide probability moves
    sources = [torch.rand(2, 64, s, s) for s in (64, 32, 16, 8)]
    with torch.no_grad():
        first = manifold.support_term(sources, None)
        manifold.guide.weight.mul_(3)
        second = manifold.support_term(sources, None)
    assert torch.allclose(first, second)
    assert first.shape == (2, 224)


def test_manifold_module_end_to_end():
    model = CASANet(module="manifold", tar_variant="cg").eval()
    image = torch.randn(2, 6, 256, 256)
    with torch.no_grad():
        pyramid = model(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
    assert [tuple(p.shape) for p in pyramid] == [(2, 1, 256, 256)] * 4
    assert torch.isfinite(pyramid[0]).all()
    folded = copy.deepcopy(model).switch_to_deploy().eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        folded_pyramid = folded(image[:, :3], image[:, 3:])
    difference = max(float((a - b).abs().max()) for a, b in zip(pyramid, folded_pyramid))
    assert difference <= 2e-4, difference
    for module, tar in (("manifold", "reparam"), ("difference", "reparam"), ("manifold", "plain")):
        try:
            CASANet(module=module, tar_variant=tar)
        except ValueError:
            pass
        else:
            raise AssertionError(f"{module} must reject tar_variant={tar}")


def test_manifold_context_reads_the_difference_stream():
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    ctx = ManifoldContext()
    ctx.eval()
    t = [torch.randn(1, 64, s, s) for s in (64, 32, 16, 8)]
    d = [torch.randn(1, 64, s, s) for s in (64, 32, 16, 8)]
    with torch.no_grad():
        proto_a = ctx.prototype_features(t, d)
        other = [x + 1.5 for x in d]
        proto_b = ctx.prototype_features(t, other)
    assert all(torch.allclose(a, b) is False for a, b in zip(proto_a, proto_b)), \
        "the prototypes ignore the difference stream"
    assert all(torch.allclose(p, channel_norm(ctx.norm_diff, q))
               for p, q in zip(proto_a, d)), "prototypes are not the normalised difference"
    try:
        ctx.prototype_features(t, None)
    except ValueError:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError("a missing difference stream must raise")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    for name, fn in sorted(globals().items()):
        if name.startswith("test_") and callable(fn):
            fn()
            print(f"[OK] {name}")
    print("[OK] run4 module checks passed")
