# FILE: models/model\metric_tool.py
================================================================================
import numpy as np


###################       metrics      ###################
class AverageMeter(object):
    """Computes and stores the average and current value"""

    def __init__(self):
        self.initialized = False
        self.val = None
        self.avg = None
        self.sum = None
        self.count = None

    def initialize(self, val, weight):
        self.val = val
        self.avg = val
        self.sum = val * weight
        self.count = weight
        self.initialized = True

    def update(self, val, weight=1):
        if not self.initialized:
            self.initialize(val, weight)
        else:
            self.add(val, weight)

    def add(self, val, weight):
        self.val = val
        self.sum += val * weight
        self.count += weight
        self.avg = self.sum / self.count

    def value(self):
        return self.val

    def average(self):
        return self.avg

    def get_scores(self):
        scores_dict = cm2score(self.sum)
        return scores_dict

    def clear(self):
        self.initialized = False


###################      cm metrics      ###################
class ConfuseMatrixMeter(AverageMeter):
    """Computes and stores the average and current value"""

    def __init__(self, n_class):
        super(ConfuseMatrixMeter, self).__init__()
        self.n_class = n_class

    def update_cm(self, pr, gt, weight=1):
        """获得当前混淆矩阵，并计算当前F1得分，并更新混淆矩阵"""
        val = get_confuse_matrix(num_classes=self.n_class, label_gts=gt, label_preds=pr)
        self.update(val, weight)
        current_score = cm2F1(val)
        return current_score

    def get_scores(self):
        scores_dict = cm2score(self.sum)
        return scores_dict


def harmonic_mean(xs):
    harmonic_mean = len(xs) / sum((x + 1e-6) ** -1 for x in xs)
    return harmonic_mean


def cm2F1(confusion_matrix):
    hist = confusion_matrix
    tp = hist[1, 1]
    fn = hist[1, 0]
    fp = hist[0, 1]
    tn = hist[0, 0]
    # recall
    recall = tp / (tp + fn + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # precision
    precision = tp / (tp + fp + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # F1 score
    f1 = 2 * recall * precision / (recall + precision + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    return f1


def cm2score(confusion_matrix):
    hist = confusion_matrix
    tp = hist[1, 1]
    fn = hist[1, 0]
    fp = hist[0, 1]
    tn = hist[0, 0]
    # acc
    oa = (tp + tn) / (tp + fn + fp + tn + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # recall
    recall = tp / (tp + fn + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # precision
    precision = tp / (tp + fp + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # F1 score
    f1 = 2 * recall * precision / (recall + precision + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # IoU
    iou = tp / (tp + fp + fn + np.finfo(np.float32).eps)
    # pre
    pre = ((tp + fn) * (tp + fp) + (tn + fp) * (tn + fn)) / (tp + fp + tn + fn) ** 2
    # kappa
    kappa = (oa - pre) / (1 - pre)
    score_dict = {'Kappa': kappa, 'IoU': iou, 'F1': f1, 'OA': oa, 'recall': recall, 'precision': precision, 'Pre': pre}
    return score_dict


def get_confuse_matrix(num_classes, label_gts, label_preds):
    """计算一组预测的混淆矩阵"""

    def __fast_hist(label_gt, label_pred):
        """
        Collect values for Confusion Matrix
        For reference, please see: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Confusion_matrix
        :param label_gt: <np.array> ground-truth
        :param label_pred: <np.array> prediction
        :return: <np.ndarray> values for confusion matrix
        """
        mask = (label_gt >= 0) & (label_gt < num_classes)
        hist = np.bincount(num_classes * label_gt[mask].astype(int) + label_pred[mask],
                           minlength=num_classes ** 2).reshape(num_classes, num_classes)
        return hist

    confusion_matrix = np.zeros((num_classes, num_classes))
    for lt, lp in zip(label_gts, label_preds):
        confusion_matrix += __fast_hist(lt.flatten(), lp.flatten())
    return confusion_matrix

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\mobilenet_v2.py
================================================================================
import torch.nn as nn
import torch


class ConvBNReLU(nn.Sequential):
    def __init__(self, in_planes, out_planes, kernel_size=3, stride=1, groups=1, dilation=1):
        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2
        if dilation != 1:
            padding = dilation
        super(ConvBNReLU, self).__init__(
            nn.Conv2d(in_planes, out_planes, kernel_size, stride, padding, groups=groups, dilation=dilation,
                      bias=False),
            nn.BatchNorm2d(out_planes),
            nn.ReLU6(inplace=True)
        )


class InvertedResidual(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, inp, oup, stride, expand_ratio, dilation=1):
        super(InvertedResidual, self).__init__()
        self.stride = stride
        assert stride in [1, 2]

        hidden_dim = int(round(inp * expand_ratio))
        self.use_res_connect = self.stride == 1 and inp == oup

        layers = []
        if expand_ratio != 1:
            # pw
            layers.append(ConvBNReLU(inp, hidden_dim, kernel_size=1))
        layers.extend([
            # dw
            ConvBNReLU(hidden_dim, hidden_dim, stride=stride, groups=hidden_dim, dilation=dilation),
            # pw-linear
            nn.Conv2d(hidden_dim, oup, 1, 1, 0, bias=False),
            nn.BatchNorm2d(oup),
        ])
        self.conv = nn.Sequential(*layers)

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.use_res_connect:
            return x + self.conv(x)
        else:
            return self.conv(x)


class MobileNetV2(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, pretrained=None, num_classes=1000, width_mult=1.0):
        super(MobileNetV2, self).__init__()
        block = InvertedResidual
        input_channel = 32
        inverted_residual_setting = [
            # t, c, n, s, d
            [1, 16, 1, 1, 1],
            [6, 24, 2, 2, 1],
            [6, 32, 3, 2, 1],
            [6, 64, 4, 2, 1],
            [6, 96, 3, 1, 1],
            [6, 160, 3, 2, 1],
            [6, 320, 1, 1, 1],
        ]

        # building first layer
        input_channel = int(input_channel * width_mult)
        features = [ConvBNReLU(3, input_channel, stride=2)]
        # building inverted residual blocks
        for t, c, n, s, d in inverted_residual_setting:
            output_channel = int(c * width_mult)
            for i in range(n):
                stride = s if i == 0 else 1
                dilation = d if i == 0 else 1
                features.append(block(input_channel, output_channel, stride, expand_ratio=t, dilation=d))
                input_channel = output_channel
        # make it nn.Sequential
        self.features = nn.Sequential(*features)

        # weight initialization
        for m in self.modules():
            if isinstance(m, nn.Conv2d):
                nn.init.kaiming_normal_(m.weight, mode='fan_out')
                if m.bias is not None:
                    nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)
            elif isinstance(m, nn.BatchNorm2d):
                nn.init.ones_(m.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)
            elif isinstance(m, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(m.weight, 0, 0.01)
                nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)

    def forward(self, x):
        res = []
        for idx, m in enumerate(self.features):
            x = m(x)
            if idx in [1, 3, 6, 13, 17]:
                res.append(x)
        return res


def mobilenet_v2(pretrained_path=None):
    model = MobileNetV2()
    if pretrained_path:
        state = torch.load(pretrained_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
        missing, unexpected = model.load_state_dict(state, strict=False)
        if missing:
            raise RuntimeError(f"MobileNetV2 missing pretrained tensors: {missing}")
        print(f"[PRETRAIN-LOAD] MobileNetV2 tensors={len(model.state_dict())} missing=0 unexpected={unexpected}", flush=True)
    return model

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\str_dcr.py
================================================================================
"""Top-down DCR decoder with foldable local refinement.

TAR features at 8/16/32/64 spatial resolution are fused from coarse to fine.
The four decoder outputs support deep supervision at input resolution.
The ``plain`` variant keeps the same topology but replaces every foldable
multi-branch re-parameterized layer with a single convolution plus batch norm.
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.str_reparam import (
    RepDW3,
    RepPW1x1,
    RepPairFuse1x1,
    fold_conv_bn,
    switch_module_to_deploy,
)


class RepLocalBlock(nn.Module):
    """Local refinement block: DW3 (residual) -> SiLU -> PW1 (residual) -> SiLU."""

    def __init__(self, dim, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.dw = RepDW3(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
        self.pw = RepPW1x1(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, x):
        x = self.act(self.dw(x))
        x = self.act(self.pw(x))
        return x

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.dw.switch_to_deploy()
        self.pw.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


class PlainLocalBlock(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: plain depthwise 3x3 and pointwise 1x1 without multi-branch re-parameterization."""

    def __init__(self, dim, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.deploy = deploy
        self.dw = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, 3, padding=1, groups=dim, bias=deploy)
        self.pw = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, 1, bias=deploy)
        if not deploy:
            self.bn_dw = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)
            self.bn_pw = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.deploy:
            return self.act(self.pw(self.act(self.dw(x))))
        x = self.act(self.bn_dw(self.dw(x)))
        return self.act(self.bn_pw(self.pw(x)))

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel_dw, bias_dw = fold_conv_bn(self.dw.weight, None, self.bn_dw)
        kernel_pw, bias_pw = fold_conv_bn(self.pw.weight, None, self.bn_pw)
        self.dw = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.dim, 3, padding=1, groups=self.dim, bias=True)
        self.pw = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.dim, 1, bias=True)
        self.dw.weight.data, self.dw.bias.data = kernel_dw.float(), bias_dw.float()
        self.pw.weight.data, self.pw.bias.data = kernel_pw.float(), bias_pw.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("bn_dw", "bn_pw"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


class PlainFuse1x1(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: plain concat 1x1 fusion without sum and signed-difference branches."""

    def __init__(self, channels, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.channels = channels
        self.deploy = deploy
        self.fuse = nn.Conv2d(2 * channels, channels, 1, bias=deploy)
        if not deploy:
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)

    def forward(self, left, right):
        fused = self.fuse(torch.cat((left, right), 1))
        return fused if self.deploy else self.bn(fused)

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = fold_conv_bn(self.fuse.weight, None, self.bn)
        self.fuse = nn.Conv2d(2 * self.channels, self.channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.fuse.weight.data, self.fuse.bias.data = kernel.float(), bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        if hasattr(self, "bn"):
            del self.bn
        return self


class SingleLevelDecoder(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: one learned decoding level, no multi-scale fusion stack.

    Only the coarsest TAR feature goes through the projection and local mixer;
    finer levels are added on top-down as a parameter-free residual, so the
    finer TAR stages still receive gradient instead of becoming dead weights.
    """

    def __init__(self, dim=64, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.deploy = deploy
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, 1, bias=deploy)
        if not deploy:
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)
        self.block = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, feats):
        return self.forward_pyramid(feats)[0]

    def forward_pyramid(self, feats):
        t1, t2, t3, t4 = feats
        x = self.proj(t4)
        if not self.deploy:
            x = self.bn(x)
        d4 = self.block(self.act(x))
        d3 = t3 + F.interpolate(d4, size=t3.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        d2 = t2 + F.interpolate(d3, size=t2.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        d1 = t1 + F.interpolate(d2, size=t1.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        return [d1, d2, d3, d4]

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = fold_conv_bn(self.proj.weight, None, self.bn)
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.dim, 1, bias=True)
        self.proj.weight.data, self.proj.bias.data = kernel.float(), bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        if hasattr(self, "bn"):
            del self.bn
        self.block.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


def build_decoder(dim=64, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False, variant="reparam"):
    """Build the decoder for a given ablation variant."""
    if variant == "none":
        return SingleLevelDecoder(dim, deploy=deploy)
    return DCRDecoder(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy,
                      variant=variant)


class DCRDecoder(nn.Module):
    """Top-down multi-scale decoder over TAR features [t1, t2, t3, t4] (dim ch)."""

    def __init__(self, dim=64, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False, variant="reparam"):
        super().__init__()
        if variant not in ("reparam", "plain"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown decoder variant: {variant}")
        self.dim = dim
        self.use_aux = use_aux
        self.variant = variant
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

        if variant == "reparam":
            self.fuse3 = RepPairFuse1x1(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.block3 = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.fuse2 = RepPairFuse1x1(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.block2 = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.fuse1 = RepPairFuse1x1(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.block1 = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
            self.refine = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
        else:
            self.fuse3 = PlainFuse1x1(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.block3 = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.fuse2 = PlainFuse1x1(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.block2 = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.fuse1 = PlainFuse1x1(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.block1 = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
            self.refine = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)

    def forward(self, feats):
        return self.forward_pyramid(feats)[0]

    def forward_pyramid(self, feats):
        """Return the four decoder scales for deep supervision."""
        t1, t2, t3, t4 = feats

        d4 = t4

        u4 = F.interpolate(d4, size=t3.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        d3 = self.block3(self.act(self.fuse3(t3, u4)))

        u3 = F.interpolate(d3, size=t2.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        d2 = self.block2(self.act(self.fuse2(t2, u3)))

        u2 = F.interpolate(d2, size=t1.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
        d1 = self.block1(self.act(self.fuse1(t1, u2)))

        d1 = self.refine(d1)
        return [d1, d2, d3, d4]

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        switch_module_to_deploy(self)
        return self

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\str_reparam.py
================================================================================
"""Foldable depthwise, pointwise and paired-feature convolution blocks.

Training branches use separate BatchNorm layers and learnable residuals.
Kernel and bias composition uses FP64 before one FP32 cast for deployment.
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F


def fold_conv_bn(weight, bias, bn):
    """Fold one Conv (weight, bias) + BatchNorm into (W', b').

    Returns float64 tensors: ALL branch composition must stay in float64 and
    cast to FP32 once, at the final deploy-conv assignment.
    """
    C = weight.shape[0]
    eps = bn.eps if bn.eps is not None else 1e-5
    gamma = bn.weight.detach().double()
    beta = bn.bias.detach().double()
    mu = bn.running_mean.detach().double()
    var = bn.running_var.detach().double()
    std = torch.sqrt(var + eps)
    t = gamma / std
    bd = bias.detach().double() if bias is not None else torch.zeros(C, dtype=torch.float64, device=weight.device)
    W = weight.detach().double() * t.view(C, 1, 1, 1)
    b = beta + (bd - mu) * t
    return W, b


def pad_1x3_to_3x3(w):
    return F.pad(w, (0, 0, 1, 1))


def pad_3x1_to_3x3(w):
    return F.pad(w, (1, 1, 0, 0))


def identity_dw_kernel(channels, k=3, device=None, dtype=torch.float32):
    kernel = torch.zeros(channels, 1, k, k, device=device, dtype=dtype)
    kernel[:, 0, k // 2, k // 2] = 1.0
    return kernel


def identity_1x1_kernel(channels, device=None, dtype=torch.float32):
    return torch.eye(channels, device=device, dtype=dtype).view(channels, channels, 1, 1)


class RepDW3(nn.Module):
    """Depthwise Rep 3x3: DW3x3 + DW1x3 + DW3x1 (each with own BN) + alpha*x -> single DW3x3."""

    def __init__(self, channels, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.channels = channels
        self.use_aux = use_aux
        self.use_residual = use_residual
        self.deploy = deploy
        if deploy:
            self.dw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, 1, 1, groups=channels, bias=True)
        else:
            self.dw3 = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, 1, 1, groups=channels, bias=False)
            self.bn3 = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.dw3.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")
            if self.use_aux:
                # Keep auxiliary construction independent of the shared initialization stream.
                from torch.nn.utils import skip_init
                self.dw13 = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, channels, channels, (1, 3), 1, (0, 1), groups=channels, bias=False)
                self.bn13 = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
                self.dw31 = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, channels, channels, (3, 1), 1, (1, 0), groups=channels, bias=False)
                self.bn31 = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.dw13.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.dw31.weight)
            if self.use_residual:
                self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.deploy:
            return self.dw(x)
        y = self.bn3(self.dw3(x))
        if self.use_aux:
            y = y + self.bn13(self.dw13(x)) + self.bn31(self.dw31(x))
        if self.use_residual:
            y = y + self.alpha * x
        return y

    def get_equivalent_kernel_bias(self):
        W3, b3 = fold_conv_bn(self.dw3.weight, None, self.bn3)
        k = W3
        b = b3
        if self.use_aux:
            W13, b13 = fold_conv_bn(self.dw13.weight, None, self.bn13)
            W31, b31 = fold_conv_bn(self.dw31.weight, None, self.bn31)
            k = k + pad_1x3_to_3x3(W13) + pad_3x1_to_3x3(W31)
            b = b + b13 + b31
        if self.use_residual:
            k = k + self.alpha.detach().double() * identity_dw_kernel(
                self.channels, 3, device=k.device, dtype=torch.float64)
        return k, b

    def branch_stats(self):
        s = {"dw3": self.dw3.weight.norm().item()}
        if self.use_aux:
            s["dw13"] = self.dw13.weight.norm().item()
            s["dw31"] = self.dw31.weight.norm().item()
        if self.use_residual:
            s["alpha"] = self.alpha.item()
        return s

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = self.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
        self.dw = nn.Conv2d(self.channels, self.channels, 3, 1, 1, groups=self.channels, bias=True)
        self.dw.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.dw.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("dw13", "dw31", "bn3", "bn13", "bn31", "dw3", "alpha"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


class RepPW1x1(nn.Module):
    """Pointwise Rep 1x1: main + serial low-rank + diag (each BN) + alpha*x -> single 1x1."""

    def __init__(self, channels, r=None, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.channels = channels
        self.r = r if r is not None else max(1, channels // 4)
        self.use_aux = use_aux
        self.use_residual = use_residual
        self.deploy = deploy
        if deploy:
            self.pw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=True)
        else:
            self.pw_main = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
            self.bn_main = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.pw_main.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")
            if self.use_aux:
                # Keep auxiliary construction independent of the shared initialization stream.
                from torch.nn.utils import skip_init
                self.pw1 = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, channels, self.r, 1, bias=False)
                self.pw2 = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, self.r, channels, 1, bias=False)
                self.bn_lr = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
                self.diag = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(channels))
                _g = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0x5F1)
                nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.pw1.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu", generator=_g)
                nn.init.normal_(self.pw2.weight, mean=0.0, std=1e-3, generator=_g)
                # Zero-initialized normalization keeps the serial branch inactive at initialization.
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bn_lr.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.bn_lr.bias)
            if self.use_residual:
                self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.deploy:
            return self.pw(x)
        y = self.bn_main(self.pw_main(x))
        if self.use_aux:
            y = y + self.bn_lr(self.pw2(self.pw1(x))) + self.diag.view(1, -1, 1, 1) * x
        if self.use_residual:
            y = y + self.alpha * x
        return y

    def get_equivalent_kernel_bias(self):
        C = self.channels
        W, b = fold_conv_bn(self.pw_main.weight, None, self.bn_main)
        if self.use_aux:
            W2 = self.pw2.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()  # (C, r)
            W1 = self.pw1.weight[:, :, 0, 0].double()  # (r, C)
            W_serial = (W2 @ W1).view(C, C, 1, 1)      # float64 compose
            W_lr, b_lr = fold_conv_bn(W_serial, None, self.bn_lr)
            D = torch.diag(self.diag.detach().double()).view(C, C, 1, 1)
            W = W + W_lr + D
            b = b + b_lr
        if self.use_residual:
            W = W + self.alpha.detach().double() * identity_1x1_kernel(
                C, device=W.device, dtype=torch.float64)
        return W, b

    def branch_stats(self):
        s = {"main": self.pw_main.weight.norm().item()}
        if self.use_aux:
            s["pw1"] = self.pw1.weight.norm().item()
            s["pw2"] = self.pw2.weight.norm().item()
        if self.use_residual:
            s["alpha"] = self.alpha.item()
        return s

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = self.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
        self.pw = nn.Conv2d(self.channels, self.channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.pw.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.pw.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("pw1", "pw2", "diag", "bn_main", "bn_lr", "pw_main", "alpha"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


class RepPairFuse1x1(nn.Module):
    """Cross-scale fusion Rep 1x1: concat + sum + diff (each BN) + alpha*L -> single 1x1."""

    def __init__(self, channels, use_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.channels = channels
        self.use_aux = use_aux
        self.use_residual = use_residual
        self.deploy = deploy
        if deploy:
            self.fuse = nn.Conv2d(2 * channels, channels, 1, bias=True)
        else:
            self.fuse_c = nn.Conv2d(2 * channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
            self.bn_c = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.fuse_c.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")
            if self.use_aux:
                # Keep auxiliary construction independent of the shared initialization stream.
                from torch.nn.utils import skip_init
                self.fuse_s = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
                self.bn_s = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
                self.fuse_d = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
                self.bn_d = nn.BatchNorm2d(channels)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.fuse_s.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.fuse_d.weight)
            if self.use_residual:
                self.alpha = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1))

    def forward(self, L, H):
        if self.deploy:
            return self.fuse(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1))
        y = self.bn_c(self.fuse_c(torch.cat([L, H], dim=1)))
        if self.use_aux:
            y = y + self.bn_s(self.fuse_s(L + H)) + self.bn_d(self.fuse_d(H - L))
        if self.use_residual:
            y = y + self.alpha * L
        return y

    def get_equivalent_kernel_bias(self):
        C = self.channels
        W_c, b_c = fold_conv_bn(self.fuse_c.weight, None, self.bn_c)
        W_cL = W_c[:, :C]
        W_cH = W_c[:, C:]
        if self.use_aux:
            W_s, b_s = fold_conv_bn(self.fuse_s.weight, None, self.bn_s)
            W_d, b_d = fold_conv_bn(self.fuse_d.weight, None, self.bn_d)
            W_L = W_cL + W_s - W_d
            W_H = W_cH + W_s + W_d
            b = b_c + b_s + b_d
        else:
            W_L, W_H = W_cL, W_cH
            b = b_c
        if self.use_residual:
            W_L = W_L + self.alpha.detach().double() * identity_1x1_kernel(
                C, device=W_L.device, dtype=torch.float64)
        W = torch.cat([W_L, W_H], dim=1)
        return W, b

    def branch_stats(self):
        s = {"concat": self.fuse_c.weight.norm().item()}
        if self.use_aux:
            s["sum"] = self.fuse_s.weight.norm().item()
            s["diff"] = self.fuse_d.weight.norm().item()
        if self.use_residual:
            s["alpha"] = self.alpha.item()
        return s

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = self.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
        self.fuse = nn.Conv2d(2 * self.channels, self.channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.fuse.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.fuse.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("fuse_s", "fuse_d", "bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d", "fuse_c", "alpha"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


def switch_module_to_deploy(module):
    for m in module.modules():
        if m is not module and hasattr(m, "switch_to_deploy"):
            m.switch_to_deploy()
    return module

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\str_tar.py
================================================================================
"""Four-scale temporal fusion with foldable concat, sum and signed-difference branches.

The ``plain`` variant keeps the same stage topology (temporal 1x1 -> local block)
but uses a single concat 1x1 convolution plus batch norm instead of the foldable
three-branch re-parameterization.
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F

from model.str_reparam import fold_conv_bn, switch_module_to_deploy
from model.str_dcr import PlainLocalBlock, RepLocalBlock
from model.edge_supervision import EdgeHead


def embed_depthwise_kernel(weight, target):
    """Place a k x k depthwise kernel in the centre of a target x target kernel."""
    k = weight.shape[-1]
    if k == target:
        return weight
    offset = (target - k) // 2
    out = torch.zeros(weight.shape[0], weight.shape[1], target, target,
                      dtype=weight.dtype, device=weight.device)
    out[..., offset:offset + k, offset:offset + k] = weight
    return out


class TemporalRep1x1(nn.Module):
    """Bi-temporal 1x1 re-parameterization: concat + sum + signed-diff (each BN) -> single 1x1."""

    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, use_aux=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_channels = in_channels
        self.out_channels = out_channels
        self.use_aux = use_aux
        self.deploy = deploy
        if deploy:
            self.proj = nn.Conv2d(2 * in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=True)
        else:
            self.proj_c = nn.Conv2d(2 * in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=False)
            self.bn_c = nn.BatchNorm2d(out_channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.proj_c.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")
            if self.use_aux:
                # Keep auxiliary construction independent of the shared initialization stream.
                from torch.nn.utils import skip_init
                self.proj_s = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=False)
                self.bn_s = nn.BatchNorm2d(out_channels)
                self.proj_d = skip_init(nn.Conv2d, in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=False)
                self.bn_d = nn.BatchNorm2d(out_channels)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.proj_s.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(self.proj_d.weight)

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        if self.deploy:
            return self.proj(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1))
        y = self.bn_c(self.proj_c(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1)))
        if self.use_aux:
            y = y + self.bn_s(self.proj_s(P + Q)) + self.bn_d(self.proj_d(Q - P))
        return y

    def get_equivalent_kernel_bias(self):
        C = self.in_channels
        W_c, b_c = fold_conv_bn(self.proj_c.weight, None, self.bn_c)
        W_P = W_c[:, :C]
        W_Q = W_c[:, C:]
        b = b_c
        if self.use_aux:
            W_s, b_s = fold_conv_bn(self.proj_s.weight, None, self.bn_s)
            W_d, b_d = fold_conv_bn(self.proj_d.weight, None, self.bn_d)
            W_P = W_P + W_s - W_d
            W_Q = W_Q + W_s + W_d
            b = b + b_s + b_d
        W = torch.cat([W_P, W_Q], dim=1)
        return W, b

    def branch_stats(self):
        s = {"concat": self.proj_c.weight.norm().item()}
        if self.use_aux:
            s["sum"] = self.proj_s.weight.norm().item()
            s["diff"] = self.proj_d.weight.norm().item()
        return s

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = self.get_equivalent_kernel_bias()
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(2 * self.in_channels, self.out_channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.proj.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.proj.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("proj_s", "proj_d", "bn_c", "bn_s", "bn_d", "proj_c"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


class TARStage(nn.Module):
    """One scale: temporal 1x1 projection, SiLU and local refinement."""

    def __init__(self, in_channels, dim, use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True,
                 use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.temporal = TemporalRep1x1(in_channels, dim, use_aux=use_temporal_aux, deploy=deploy)
        self.block = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_dcr_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        x = self.act(self.temporal(P, Q))
        return self.block(x)

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.temporal.switch_to_deploy()
        self.block.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


class PlainTemporal1x1(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: single concat 1x1 convolution plus batch norm, no auxiliary branches."""

    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_channels = in_channels
        self.out_channels = out_channels
        self.deploy = deploy
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(2 * in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=deploy)
        if not deploy:
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(out_channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.proj.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        fused = self.proj(torch.cat([P, Q], dim=1))
        return fused if self.deploy else self.bn(fused)

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = fold_conv_bn(self.proj.weight, None, self.bn)
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(2 * self.in_channels, self.out_channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.proj.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.proj.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        if hasattr(self, "bn"):
            del self.bn
        return self


class PlainTARStage(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: one scale with plain temporal fusion and plain local refinement."""

    def __init__(self, in_channels, dim, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.temporal = PlainTemporal1x1(in_channels, dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.block = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        return self.block(self.act(self.temporal(P, Q)))

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.temporal.switch_to_deploy()
        self.block.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


class DifferenceTemporal1x1(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: fixed absolute temporal difference plus one 1x1 read-out.

    Removes the learned bi-temporal mixing entirely: the two dates are compared
    by |P - Q| and only a per-channel projection is learned.
    """

    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.in_channels = in_channels
        self.out_channels = out_channels
        self.deploy = deploy
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(in_channels, out_channels, 1, bias=deploy)
        if not deploy:
            self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(out_channels)
            nn.init.kaiming_normal_(self.proj.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        fused = self.proj((P - Q).abs())
        return fused if self.deploy else self.bn(fused)

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        kernel, bias = fold_conv_bn(self.proj.weight, None, self.bn)
        self.proj = nn.Conv2d(self.in_channels, self.out_channels, 1, bias=True)
        self.proj.weight.data = kernel.float()
        self.proj.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        if hasattr(self, "bn"):
            del self.bn
        return self


class DifferenceTARStage(nn.Module):
    """Ablation: one scale with fixed absolute difference and plain local refinement.

    A single learned 1x1 mixes the two dates, so ``forward_with_difference``
    exposes the mixed difference stream for the difference-conditioned retrieval
    without any multi-scale pooling on top of it.
    """

    def __init__(self, in_channels, dim, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.temporal = DifferenceTemporal1x1(in_channels, dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.block = PlainLocalBlock(dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward_with_difference(self, P, Q):
        detail = self.temporal(P, Q)
        return self.block(self.act(detail)), detail

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        return self.forward_with_difference(P, Q)[0]

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.temporal.switch_to_deploy()
        self.block.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


class RepSPPBlock(nn.Module):
    """Multi-scale depthwise pooling that folds into one k x k depthwise convolution.

    Parallel 1x1 / 3x3 / 5x5 / 7x7 depthwise branches (each with its own batch
    norm) are summed, refined by a 1x1 pointwise convolution and added
    residually. At deployment every branch folds into a single 7x7 depthwise
    kernel plus one 1x1 convolution, so the training-time multi-scale path costs
    nothing extra at inference beyond a wider depthwise kernel.
    """

    def __init__(self, dim, kernels=(1, 3, 5, 7), use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.dim = dim
        self.kernels = tuple(kernels)
        self.max_kernel = max(self.kernels)
        self.use_residual = use_residual
        self.deploy = deploy
        if deploy:
            self.dw = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, self.max_kernel, padding=self.max_kernel // 2,
                                groups=dim, bias=True)
            self.pw = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, 1, bias=True)
            return
        self.dw_branches = nn.ModuleList(
            nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, k, padding=k // 2, groups=dim, bias=False) for k in self.kernels)
        self.bn_branches = nn.ModuleList(nn.BatchNorm2d(dim) for _ in self.kernels)
        for conv, bn, k in zip(self.dw_branches, self.bn_branches, self.kernels):
            if k == 1:
                nn.init.dirac_(conv.weight)
            else:
                # RepVGG-style: the branch starts switched off through its BN scale,
                # which keeps the initial function equal to the cheap 1x1 path while
                # gradients can still open the wider kernels.
                nn.init.kaiming_normal_(conv.weight, mode="fan_out", nonlinearity="relu")
                nn.init.zeros_(bn.weight)
                nn.init.zeros_(bn.bias)
        self.pw = nn.Conv2d(dim, dim, 1, bias=False)
        nn.init.dirac_(self.pw.weight)
        self.bn = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        if self.deploy:
            y = self.pw(self.dw(x))
        else:
            pooled = self.bn_branches[0](self.dw_branches[0](x))
            for conv, bn in zip(self.dw_branches[1:], self.bn_branches[1:]):
                pooled = pooled + bn(conv(x))
            y = self.bn(self.pw(pooled))
        return x + y if self.use_residual else y

    def get_equivalent_kernel(self):
        target = self.max_kernel
        device = self.dw_branches[0].weight.device
        weight = torch.zeros(self.dim, 1, target, target, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
        bias = torch.zeros(self.dim, dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
        for conv, bn in zip(self.dw_branches, self.bn_branches):
            kernel, shift = fold_conv_bn(conv.weight, None, bn)
            weight = weight + embed_depthwise_kernel(kernel, target)
            bias = bias + shift
        return weight, bias

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        if self.deploy:
            return self
        weight, bias = self.get_equivalent_kernel()
        pointwise, pointwise_bias = fold_conv_bn(self.pw.weight, None, self.bn)
        self.dw = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.dim, self.max_kernel,
                            padding=self.max_kernel // 2, groups=self.dim, bias=True)
        self.dw.weight.data = weight.float()
        self.dw.bias.data = bias.float()
        self.pw = nn.Conv2d(self.dim, self.dim, 1, bias=True)
        self.pw.weight.data = pointwise.float()
        self.pw.bias.data = pointwise_bias.float()
        self.deploy = True
        for name in ("dw_branches", "bn_branches", "bn"):
            if hasattr(self, name):
                delattr(self, name)
        return self


class SPPTARStage(nn.Module):
    """One scale: learned temporal mixing plus a multi-scale difference branch.

    The fused feature keeps the foldable concat/sum/signed-difference mixing; the
    absolute temporal difference is passed through the multi-scale depthwise
    pooling so small changed structures survive pooling. The pooled difference is
    added to the fused feature and also returned as a separate stream for the
    difference-conditioned retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(self, in_channels, dim, use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True,
                 use_residual=True, deploy=False, kernels=(1, 3, 5, 7)):
        super().__init__()
        self.temporal = TemporalRep1x1(in_channels, dim, use_aux=use_temporal_aux, deploy=deploy)
        self.difference = DifferenceTemporal1x1(in_channels, dim, deploy=deploy)
        self.spp = RepSPPBlock(dim, kernels=kernels, deploy=deploy)
        self.block = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_dcr_aux, use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)
        self.act = nn.SiLU()

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        detail = self.spp(self.difference(P, Q))
        fused = self.act(self.temporal(P, Q)) + detail
        return self.block(fused), detail

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        self.temporal.switch_to_deploy()
        self.difference.switch_to_deploy()
        self.spp.switch_to_deploy()
        self.block.switch_to_deploy()
        return self


class LocalContrast(nn.Module):
    """Divide every channel by its local mean magnitude (exposure invariant)."""

    def __init__(self, kernel=7):
        super().__init__()
        self.kernel = kernel

    def forward(self, x):
        scale = F.avg_pool2d(x.abs(), self.kernel, 1, self.kernel // 2).clamp_min(1e-3)
        return x / scale


class ChangeGateTARStage(nn.Module):
    """Content path plus a signed, exposure-normalised change path.

    ``content = SiLU(1x1[P, Q])`` keeps appearance; ``delta = 1x1[d+, d-]`` keeps
    the signed change after dividing both dates by their local mean magnitude, so
    a global exposure shift cancels while a real change survives. The change gate
    modulates the content multiplicatively and adds a zero-initialised copy of
    ``delta``; the local block is the same foldable DW+PW as elsewhere.
    """

    def __init__(self, in_channels, dim, use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True,
                 use_residual=True, deploy=False):
        super().__init__()
        self.contrast = LocalContrast(7)
        self.content = nn.Conv2d(2 * in_channels, dim, 1, bias=False)
        self.bn_content = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)
        self.difference = nn.Conv2d(2 * in_channels, dim, 1, bias=False)
        self.bn_difference = nn.BatchNorm2d(dim)
        self.gate = nn.Conv2d(dim + in_channels, dim, 1)
        self.beta = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.block = RepLocalBlock(dim, use_aux=use_temporal_aux,
                                   use_residual=use_residual, deploy=deploy)

    def forward(self, P, Q):
        content = F.silu(self.bn_content(self.content(torch.cat((P, Q), dim=1))))
        p, q = self.contrast(P), self.contrast(Q)
        positive = (p - q).relu()
        negative = (q - p).relu()
        delta = self.bn_difference(self.difference(torch.cat((positive, negative), dim=1)))
        gate = self.gate(torch.cat((delta, positive + negative), dim=1)).sigmoid()
        fused = self.block(content * (1 + gate) + self.beta * delta)
        return fused, delta


class MultiScaleTAR(nn.Module):
    """Four-scale temporal fusion from paired encoder features to decoder inputs.

    Input scale order matches STR convention: stage1 (finest, 64x64) .. stage4
    (coarsest, 8x8); output list [t1, t2, t3, t4] feeds DCRDecoder.
    """

    def __init__(self, encoder_dims=(64, 64, 64, 64), dim=64,
                 use_temporal_aux=True, use_dcr_aux=True, use_residual=True, deploy=False,
                 variant="reparam", return_difference=False, edge_supervision=False):
        super().__init__()
        if variant not in ("reparam", "plain", "diff", "spp", "spp1", "cg"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown TAR variant: {variant}")
        self.variant = variant
        self.return_difference = return_difference
        if variant == "reparam":
            self.stage1 = TARStage(encoder_dims[0], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                   use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage2 = TARStage(encoder_dims[1], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                   use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage3 = TARStage(encoder_dims[2], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                   use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage4 = TARStage(encoder_dims[3], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                   use_residual, deploy)
        elif variant == "plain":
            self.stage1 = PlainTARStage(encoder_dims[0], dim, deploy)
            self.stage2 = PlainTARStage(encoder_dims[1], dim, deploy)
            self.stage3 = PlainTARStage(encoder_dims[2], dim, deploy)
            self.stage4 = PlainTARStage(encoder_dims[3], dim, deploy)
        elif variant == "diff":
            self.stage1 = DifferenceTARStage(encoder_dims[0], dim, deploy)
            self.stage2 = DifferenceTARStage(encoder_dims[1], dim, deploy)
            self.stage3 = DifferenceTARStage(encoder_dims[2], dim, deploy)
            self.stage4 = DifferenceTARStage(encoder_dims[3], dim, deploy)
        elif variant == "cg":
            self.stage1 = ChangeGateTARStage(encoder_dims[0], dim, use_temporal_aux,
                                             use_dcr_aux, use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage2 = ChangeGateTARStage(encoder_dims[1], dim, use_temporal_aux,
                                             use_dcr_aux, use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage3 = ChangeGateTARStage(encoder_dims[2], dim, use_temporal_aux,
                                             use_dcr_aux, use_residual, deploy)
            self.stage4 = ChangeGateTARStage(encoder_dims[3], dim, use_temporal_aux,
                                             use_dcr_aux, use_residual, deploy)
        else:
            # "spp" is the multi-scale pooling; "spp1" keeps the same branch
            # structure but with the 1x1 kernel only, so the gain of widening the
            # pooling window can be read off directly.
            kernels = (1, 3, 5, 7) if variant == "spp" else (1,)
            self.stage1 = SPPTARStage(encoder_dims[0], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                      use_residual, deploy, kernels=kernels)
            self.stage2 = SPPTARStage(encoder_dims[1], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                      use_residual, deploy, kernels=kernels)
            self.stage3 = SPPTARStage(encoder_dims[2], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                      use_residual, deploy, kernels=kernels)
            self.stage4 = SPPTARStage(encoder_dims[3], dim, use_temporal_aux, use_dcr_aux,
                                      use_residual, deploy, kernels=kernels)
        # one shared 1x1 head reads the propagated change evidence at every scale;
        # created last so that turning it on does not shift any other initialisation
        self.edge_head = EdgeHead(dim) if edge_supervision else None

    def forward(self, pre_feats, post_feats):
        if self.variant == "cg":
            t1, d1 = self.stage1(pre_feats[0], post_feats[0])
            t2, d2 = self.stage2(pre_feats[1], post_feats[1])
            t3, d3 = self.stage3(pre_feats[2], post_feats[2])
            t4, d4 = self.stage4(pre_feats[3], post_feats[3])
            # coarse-to-fine evidence propagation: a change seen at 8x8 seeds the
            # finer scales, so a small object inherits its parent's evidence.
            d3 = d3 + F.interpolate(d4, size=d3.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
            d2 = d2 + F.interpolate(d3, size=d2.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
            d1 = d1 + F.interpolate(d2, size=d1.shape[-2:], mode="bilinear", align_corners=False)
            fused = [t1, t2, t3, t4]
            if self.edge_head is not None and self.training:
                edges = [self.edge_head(x) for x in (d1, d2, d3, d4)]
                return fused, [d1, d2, d3, d4], edges
            if self.return_difference:
                return fused, [d1, d2, d3, d4]
            return fused
        if self.variant in ("spp", "spp1"):
            t1, d1 = self.stage1(pre_feats[0], post_feats[0])
            t2, d2 = self.stage2(pre_feats[1], post_feats[1])
            t3, d3 = self.stage3(pre_feats[2], post_feats[2])
            t4, d4 = self.stage4(pre_feats[3], post_feats[3])
            fused = [t1, t2, t3, t4]
            if self.return_difference:
                return fused, [d1, d2, d3, d4]
            return fused
        if self.variant == "diff" and self.return_difference:
            t1, d1 = self.stage1.forward_with_difference(pre_feats[0], post_feats[0])
            t2, d2 = self.stage2.forward_with_difference(pre_feats[1], post_feats[1])
            t3, d3 = self.stage3.forward_with_difference(pre_feats[2], post_feats[2])
            t4, d4 = self.stage4.forward_with_difference(pre_feats[3], post_feats[3])
            return [t1, t2, t3, t4], [d1, d2, d3, d4]
        t1 = self.stage1(pre_feats[0], post_feats[0])
        t2 = self.stage2(pre_feats[1], post_feats[1])
        t3 = self.stage3(pre_feats[2], post_feats[2])
        t4 = self.stage4(pre_feats[3], post_feats[3])
        return [t1, t2, t3, t4]

    def switch_to_deploy(self):
        switch_module_to_deploy(self)
        return self

# FILE: models/model\layers\__init__.py
