"""Change-aware compressed attention and direct Lorentz geometry."""

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\class_pooling.py
================================================================================
"""Differentiable first-order change/background pooling."""
import torch
from model.layers.pyramid_context import feature_regions


def class_assignments(probability, grid):
    positive = feature_regions(probability, grid).squeeze(-1)
    membership = torch.stack((positive, 1 - positive), 2)
    mass = membership.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    denominator = torch.where(mass > 0, mass, torch.ones_like(mass))
    weights = membership / denominator
    support = (weights * membership).sum(-1).flatten(1).clamp_min(1e-6)
    return weights, support

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\difference_context.py
================================================================================
"""Difference-conditioned cross-scale retrieval.

Keys and values are pooled from the bi-temporal absolute difference ``|P - Q|``
rather than from the fused feature, so the retrieval answers *"is there changed
content of this kind"* instead of *"what does this location look like"*. Queries
stay at full resolution on the fused feature, so localisation is unchanged and
only the knowledge side of the attention becomes change-specific.

The geometry, support prior, scale bias and centred gated readout are inherited
unchanged from :class:`HyperbolicContext`; this class only swaps the content the
prototypes are pooled from.
"""
import torch
from torch import nn

from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import HyperbolicContext
from model.layers.pyramid_context import channel_norm


class DifferenceContext(HyperbolicContext):
    """Hyperbolic retrieval whose prototype content is the temporal difference."""

    geometry = "hyperbolic_difference"
    key_mode = "project_pooled_temporal_difference_prototypes"

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=1, head_dim=32, grid_sizes=(8, 4, 4, 4),
                 support_mode="mass", kv_hierarchy="flat", freeze_tau=False,
                 edge_supervision=False, edge_detach="none"):
        super().__init__(channels, heads, head_dim, grid_sizes, support_mode,
                         kv_hierarchy, freeze_tau, edge_supervision)
        self.norm_diff = nn.LayerNorm(channels)

    def prototype_features(self, normalized, difference=None):
        if difference is None:
            raise ValueError("DifferenceContext requires the temporal difference stream")
        if len(difference) != len(normalized):
            raise ValueError("the difference stream must match the number of scales")
        return [channel_norm(self.norm_diff, d) for d in difference]

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\euclidean_context.py
================================================================================
"""Euclidean retrieval with selective prototypes and a dense self reference."""
import math

from model.layers.pyramid_context import ResidualPyramidContext, project_tokens, split_heads


class EuclideanContext(ResidualPyramidContext):
    geometry = "euclidean"

    def project_queries(self, feature):
        return split_heads(self.qk(feature), self.heads) / math.sqrt(feature.shape[1] // self.heads)

    def pool_keys(self, query, prototype, weights, grid):
        # LN precedes compression; the shared linear map commutes with weighted pooling.
        return project_tokens(self.qk, prototype, self.heads) / math.sqrt(prototype.shape[-1] // self.heads)

    @staticmethod
    def squared_distance(query, key):
        return (query.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True) + key.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                - 2 * (query @ key.transpose(-1, -2))).clamp_min(0)

    @staticmethod
    def geometry_diagnostics(query, key):
        return dict(query_norm_mean=float(query.norm(dim=-1).mean()),
                    query_norm_max=float(query.norm(dim=-1).max()))

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\hyperbolic_context.py
================================================================================
"""First-order class pooling and aligned geometric cross-scale retrieval."""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift, pairwise_squared_distance
from model.layers.class_pooling import class_assignments
from model.edge_supervision import EdgeHead
from model.layers.pyramid_context import feature_regions
from model.layers.pyramid_context import (
    attention_diagnostics, channel_norm, highpass, merge_heads,
    pool_features, project_tokens, rms, source_bias, split_heads,
)



FLAT_GRIDS = (8, 4, 4, 4)
# Nested hierarchy: every child cell covers exactly a 2x2 block of its parent.
NESTED_GRIDS = (8, 4, 2, 1)
# Median of the D0 measured `softplus(raw_temperature)` over the nine trained
# checkpoints (Run1 x 3 datasets + Run14 H/E pairs); used by --freeze-tau.
FROZEN_TAU = 0.4696


class HyperbolicContext(nn.Module):
    """Direct projected geometry, pooled K/V content and independently gated readout."""
    geometry = "hyperbolic"
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    initial_compression_power = 1
    compression_power = 1
    support_power = 1
    self_reference = False
    radius_mode = "asinh(norm(projected_features) / sqrt(head_dim))"
    key_mode = "project_same_pooled_content_as_values_then_lift"
    readout_mode = "learned_gate_times_spatially_centered_context"
    squared_distance = staticmethod(pairwise_squared_distance)

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=1, head_dim=32, grid_sizes=FLAT_GRIDS,
                 support_mode="mass", kv_hierarchy="flat", freeze_tau=False,
                 edge_supervision=False, edge_detach="retrieval"):
        super().__init__()
        if support_mode not in ("mass", "cell", "pixel", "none"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown support_mode {support_mode!r}")
        if kv_hierarchy not in ("flat", "nested"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown kv_hierarchy {kv_hierarchy!r}")
        support_mode = "mass" if support_mode == "cell" else support_mode
        if kv_hierarchy == "nested":
            grid_sizes = NESTED_GRIDS
            for parent, child in zip(grid_sizes[:-1], grid_sizes[1:]):
                if parent != 2 * child:
                    raise ValueError(f"nested grids must halve per scale, got {grid_sizes}")
        self.support_prior = support_mode
        self.kv_hierarchy = kv_hierarchy
        self.freeze_tau = bool(freeze_tau)
        self.heads = heads
        self.head_dim = head_dim
        self.grid_sizes = tuple(grid_sizes)
        self.tokens_per_scale = tuple(2 * grid ** 2 for grid in grid_sizes)
        width = heads * head_dim
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(channels, width, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(width, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.gate = nn.Conv2d(2 * width + 2, heads, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate.bias)
        self.local_dw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1,
                                  padding_mode="replicate", groups=channels, bias=False)
        self.local_out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(heads, 4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((heads,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))
        if self.freeze_tau:
            with torch.no_grad():
                self.raw_temperature.fill_(math.log(math.expm1(FROZEN_TAU)))
            self.raw_temperature.requires_grad_(False)
        self.gamma_context = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, channels))
        self.gamma_detail = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(2, channels))
        # created last so that switching the head on does not shift any other init
        self.edge_head = EdgeHead(channels) if edge_supervision else None
        if edge_detach not in ("none", "retrieval"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown edge_detach mode: {edge_detach}")
        self.edge_detach = edge_detach
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def scale_temperature(self, query_scale):
        """Temperature used at ``query_scale``; subclasses may make it per-scale."""
        return F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, self.heads, 1, 1)

    @staticmethod
    def geometric_point(projected):
        """Lift unnormalized projections; their norm determines intrinsic radius."""
        return lift(projected / math.sqrt(projected.shape[-1]))

    def project_queries(self, feature):
        projected = split_heads(self.qk(feature), self.heads)
        return self.geometric_point(projected)

    def project_keys(self, prototype):
        projected = project_tokens(self.qk, prototype, self.heads)
        occupancy = F.linear(prototype, self.guide.weight[:, :, 0, 0], self.guide.bias).sigmoid()
        return self.geometric_point(projected), occupancy

    def class_support(self, probability, grid):
        """Per-cell class prior used as ``log(support)`` in the attention logits.

        mass   (default, historical): ``sum_i w_ci * m_ci`` with ``w = m / sum_i m`` --
               a participation ratio that shrinks when a class is diluted by the
               other class inside the cell.
        pixel  : area-normalised mean membership per cell, ``mean_i m_ci`` --
               the pixel-level class fraction instead of the pooled mass.
        none   : a flat prior (``support == 1``, so ``log(support) == 0``).
        """
        weights, support = class_assignments(probability, grid)
        if self.support_prior == "mass":
            return weights, support
        if self.support_prior == "none":
            return weights, torch.ones_like(support)
        # pixel: area-normalised class fraction per cell (adaptive average pooling),
        # instead of the participation mass that shrinks under class mixing.
        change = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(probability, grid).flatten(2)
        pair = torch.stack((change, 1 - change), dim=-1)
        return weights, pair.reshape(pair.shape[0], -1).clamp_min(1e-6)

    def aggregate_nested(self, prototypes):
        """Child prototype = (own + mean of its 2x2 parent block) / 2; 0 new parameters."""
        aggregated = [prototypes[0]]
        for child in prototypes[1:]:
            parent = aggregated[-1]
            parent_grid = int(round((parent.shape[1] / 2) ** 0.5))
            child_grid = int(round((child.shape[1] / 2) ** 0.5))
            if child_grid * 2 != parent_grid:
                raise ValueError("nested aggregation expects child grid = parent grid / 2")
            batch, _, dim = parent.shape
            # Tokens are laid out as (cell_row, cell_col, prototype): two
            # prototypes per cell, so the trailing axis inside each cell is the
            # prototype index and the two spatial factors are the 2x2 split.
            blocked = parent.reshape(batch, parent_grid, parent_grid, 2, dim)
            blocked = blocked.reshape(batch, child_grid, 2, child_grid, 2, 2, dim).mean(dim=(2, 4))
            pooled = blocked.reshape(batch, -1, dim)
            aggregated.append(0.5 * (child + pooled))
        return aggregated

    def attention_logits(self, query, key, support, query_scale):
        temperature = self.scale_temperature(query_scale)
        logits = -self.squared_distance(query, key) / (2 * temperature.square())
        logits = logits + support.log()[:, None, None] + source_bias(
            self.scale_bias, query_scale, self.tokens_per_scale)
        return logits

    def attention(self, query, key, support, query_scale):
        return self.attention_logits(query, key, support, query_scale).softmax(-1)

    @staticmethod
    def geometric_change_logit(logits, height, width):
        """Log odds of change versus background evidence across all prototypes."""
        # Each region contributes its change prototype, then its background prototype.
        positive, background = logits.reshape(*logits.shape[:-1], -1, 2).unbind(-1)
        evidence = positive.logsumexp(-1) - background.logsumexp(-1)
        return evidence.mean(1).reshape(logits.shape[0], 1, height, width)

    def aggregate(self, probability, values):
        """Combine values with attention weights (Euclidean weighted mean by default)."""
        return probability @ values

    def support_term(self, sources, support):
        """Prior strength per prototype; subclasses may replace the pooled mass."""
        return support

    def readout(self, feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy,
                scale_index=None):
        height, width = feature.shape[-2:]
        retrieved = merge_heads(self.aggregate(probability, values), height, width)
        own_value = self.v(feature)
        discrepancy = retrieved - retrieved.mean((-2, -1), keepdim=True)
        retrieved_occupancy = (probability @ prototype_occupancy[:, None]).mean(1)
        retrieved_occupancy = retrieved_occupancy.transpose(1, 2).reshape(-1, 1, height, width)
        gate = self.gate(torch.cat((own_value, discrepancy, occupancy, retrieved_occupancy), 1)).sigmoid()
        gate_map = gate[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, self.head_dim, -1, -1).flatten(1, 2)
        gated = gate_map * discrepancy
        return self.out(gated), gate, retrieved, own_value, discrepancy, gated

    def prototype_features(self, normalized, difference=None):
        """Content the retrieval keys and values are built from (fused feature by default)."""
        return normalized

    def edge_logits(self, update, index):
        """Optional edge prediction read from the write-back update.

        ``edge_detach="retrieval"`` keeps the head and its loss but stops the
        gradient at the write-back, so the edge term only reaches the bridge.
        """
        if getattr(self, "edge_head", None) is None:
            return None
        if getattr(self, "edge_detach", "none") == "retrieval":
            update = update.detach()
        return self.edge_head(update)

    def forward(self, tar, difference=None, return_aux=False):
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, x) for x in tar]
        sources = self.prototype_features(normalized, difference)
        guide = [self.guide(x) for x in sources]
        occupancy = [g.sigmoid() for g in guide]
        compressed = [self.class_support(p, grid) for p, grid in zip(occupancy, self.grid_sizes)]
        prototypes = [pool_features(x, weights, grid)
                      for x, (weights, _), grid in zip(sources, compressed, self.grid_sizes)]
        if self.kv_hierarchy == "nested":
            prototypes = self.aggregate_nested(prototypes)
        queries = [self.project_queries(x) for x in normalized]
        prototype = torch.cat(prototypes, dim=1)
        key, prototype_occupancy = self.project_keys(prototype)
        values = project_tokens(self.v, prototype, self.heads)
        support = self.support_term(sources, torch.cat([item[1] for item in compressed], dim=1))
        enhanced, diagnostics, geometric_logits, edge_maps = [], {}, [], []
        for index, (original, x, p) in enumerate(zip(tar, normalized, occupancy)):
            query = queries[index]
            logits = self.attention_logits(query.flatten(2, 3), key, support, index)
            probability = logits.softmax(-1)
            if return_aux:
                geometric_logits.append(self.geometric_change_logit(logits, *x.shape[-2:]))
            update, gate, retrieved, own_value, discrepancy, gated = self.readout(
                x, probability, values, p, prototype_occupancy, index)
            context_residual = self.gamma_context[index][None, :, None, None] * update
            local_residual = torch.zeros_like(original)
            local = None
            if index < 2:
                local = self.local_out(self.local_dw(highpass(x)))
                local_residual = self.gamma_detail[index][None, :, None, None] * local
            result = original + context_residual + local_residual
            if self.training or return_aux:
                edge = self.edge_logits(update, index)
                if edge is not None:
                    edge_maps.append(edge)
            enhanced.append(result)
            if self.collect_diag:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    base = rms(original).clamp_min(1e-8)
                    radius = torch.asinh(query[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                    key_radius = torch.asinh(key[..., 1:].norm(dim=-1))
                    q_flat = query.flatten(2, 3)
                    direction_q = F.normalize(q_flat[..., 1:], dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
                    direction_k = F.normalize(key[..., 1:], dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
                    angular = (1 - direction_q @ direction_k.transpose(-1, -2)).clamp_min(0)
                    radial = (radius.flatten(2)[..., None] - key_radius[:, :, None]).abs()
                    weights, class_support = compressed[index]
                    effective = weights.square().sum(-1).clamp_min(1e-30).reciprocal()
                    diag = dict(
                        queries=x.shape[-2] * x.shape[-1], contexts=key.shape[2],
                        gamma_context=float(self.gamma_context[index].mean()),
                        gamma_context_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_context[index])),
                        gamma_detail=float(self.gamma_detail[index].mean()) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        gamma_detail_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_detail[index])) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        feature_rms=float(base), context_rms=float(rms(update)), branch_rms=float(rms(update)),
                        local_rms=float(rms(local)) if local is not None else 0.,
                        value_difference_rms=float(rms(discrepancy)),
                        gated_difference_rms=float(rms(gated)),
                        ungated_projection_rms=float(rms(F.conv2d(discrepancy, self.out.weight))),
                        residual_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual)),
                        residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual) / base),
                        context_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual) / base),
                        local_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(local_residual) / base),
                        guide_mean=float(p.mean()), class_support_mean=float(class_support.mean()),
                        compression_effective_pixels=float(effective.mean()),
                        compression_effective_pixels_change=float(effective[:, :, 0].mean()),
                        compression_effective_pixels_background=float(effective[:, :, 1].mean()),
                        class_support_min=float(class_support.min()),
                        retrieved_rms=float(rms(retrieved)), self_value_rms=float(rms(own_value)),
                        retrieved_spatial_rms=float(rms(retrieved - retrieved.mean((-2, -1), keepdim=True))),
                        retrieved_self_cosine=float(F.cosine_similarity(retrieved.flatten(1), own_value.flatten(1)).mean()),
                        qk_weight_rms=float(rms(self.qk.weight)), v_weight_rms=float(rms(self.v.weight)),
                        out_weight_rms=float(rms(self.out.weight)),
                        temperature=F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).tolist(),
                        radius_mean=float(radius.mean()), radius_max=float(radius.max()),
                        radius_cv=float(radius.std(unbiased=False) / radius.mean().clamp_min(1e-8)),
                        key_radius_mean=float(key_radius.mean()),
                        radial_gap_attention_mean=float((probability * radial).sum(-1).mean()),
                        angular_gap_attention_mean=float((probability * angular).sum(-1).mean()),
                        finite=bool(torch.isfinite(result).all()))
                    diag.update(attention_diagnostics(probability, self.tokens_per_scale))
                    diag.pop("self_mass_mean")
                    for name in ("context_mass_mean", "context_mass_min", "context_mass_max", "context_mass_std"):
                        diag.pop(name)
                    diag.update(gate_mean=float(gate.mean()), gate_min=float(gate.min()),
                                gate_max=float(gate.max()), gate_std=float(gate.std(unbiased=False)),
                                prototype_attention_mass_mean=float(probability.sum(-1).mean()),
                                adoption_source="learned_value_correction_gate")
                    diagnostics[f"stage{index+1}"] = diag
        if self.collect_diag:
            self.last_diag = diagnostics
        auxiliary = dict(guide_logits=tuple(guide),
                         edge_logits=tuple(edge_maps) if edge_maps else None)
        if return_aux:
            auxiliary["geometric_logits"] = tuple(geometric_logits)
        return enhanced, auxiliary


class MatchedEuclideanContext(HyperbolicContext):
    """Same representations and readout, with spatial Euclidean distance only."""
    geometry = "euclidean_matched"

    @staticmethod
    def squared_distance(query, key):
        q, k = query[..., 1:], key[..., 1:]
        return (q.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True) + k.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                - 2 * (q @ k.transpose(-1, -2))).clamp_min(0)

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\lorentz_ops.py
================================================================================
"""Curvature -1 geometry using unconstrained spatial Lorentz coordinates."""
import torch


def lift(space):
    """The intrinsic radius is asinh(||space||); there is no radial clipping."""
    return torch.cat(((1 + space.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)).sqrt(), space), dim=-1)


def normalize_sum(point):
    """Lorentz centroid of a positive weighted mean of unit hyperboloid points."""
    norm2 = point[..., :1].square() - point[..., 1:].square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    # Convex weights imply norm2 >= 1. Rebuild time to preserve the unit manifold.
    return lift(point[..., 1:] / norm2.clamp_min(1).sqrt())


def minkowski_barycenter(values, weights):
    """Weighted Minkowski centroid of lifted values, returned in tangent space.

    ``values`` is (..., tokens, dim) in Euclidean coordinates and ``weights`` is
    (..., tokens, 1) with convex weights. The centroid is formed on the lifted
    hyperboloid and mapped back to the tangent chart, so the aggregation itself
    happens in the manifold rather than in the Euclidean chart.
    """
    centroid = (weights * lift(values)).sum(dim=-2)
    norm2 = centroid[..., :1].square() - centroid[..., 1:].square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    # convex weights keep norm2 >= 1; re-normalising preserves the manifold
    return centroid[..., 1:] / norm2.clamp_min(1).sqrt()


def acosh1p_squared(z):
    """Squared geodesic distance with its finite analytic derivative at z=0."""
    regular_z = z.clamp_min(1e-3)
    regular = torch.log1p(regular_z + (regular_z * (regular_z + 2)).sqrt()).square()
    series = 2 * z - z.square() / 3 + 4 * z.pow(3) / 45 - z.pow(4) / 35
    return torch.where(z <= 1e-3, series, regular)


def pairwise_squared_distance(query, key):
    space_q, space_k = query[..., 1:], key[..., 1:]
    squared_chord = (space_q.square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
                     + space_k.square().sum(-1).unsqueeze(-2)
                     - 2 * (space_q @ space_k.transpose(-1, -2)))
    time_difference = query[..., :1] - key[..., :1].transpose(-1, -2)
    z = (0.5 * (squared_chord - time_difference.square())).clamp_min(0)
    return acosh1p_squared(z)

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\manifold_context.py
================================================================================
"""Manifold-valued retrieval: aggregate values on the hyperboloid.

The plain context only uses the Lorentz metric to shape attention weights; the
value average stays Euclidean. Here the aggregation itself is a Klein/Hyperboloid
barycenter, the prior comes from an independent change-evidence head, and the
write-back carries a level path in addition to the zero-mean contrast path.
"""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

from model.edge_supervision import EdgeHead
from model.layers.hyperbolic_context import FLAT_GRIDS, HyperbolicContext
from model.layers.lorentz_ops import lift
from model.layers.pyramid_context import channel_norm, merge_heads


class ManifoldContext(HyperbolicContext):
    """Change-evidence prototypes retrieved and averaged on the Lorentz model."""

    geometry = "hyperbolic_manifold"
    key_mode = "pooled_change_evidence_prototypes"
    value_geometry = "lorentz_barycenter"
    readout_mode = "lorentz_barycenter_with_level_and_position"

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=1, head_dim=32, grid_sizes=FLAT_GRIDS,
                 support_mode="mass", kv_hierarchy="flat", freeze_tau=False,
                 edge_supervision=False, edge_detach="none"):
        super().__init__(channels, heads, head_dim, grid_sizes,
                         support_mode, kv_hierarchy, freeze_tau, edge_supervision,
                         edge_detach=edge_detach)
        width = heads * head_dim
        self.scale_log_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(len(self.grid_sizes)))
        self.support_head = nn.Conv2d(channels, 2, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.support_head.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.support_head.bias)
        self.level = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(width, channels), nn.SiLU(),
                                   nn.Linear(channels, channels))
        self.level_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))
        self.gate = nn.Conv2d(2 * width + 5, heads, 1)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate.weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate.bias)
        # the change-evidence stream is normalised on its own so the prototypes are
        # built from D_s, exactly like the difference-conditioned context.
        self.norm_diff = nn.LayerNorm(channels)

    def prototype_features(self, normalized, difference=None):
        """Keys and values are pooled from the change evidence, not the content."""
        if difference is None:
            raise ValueError("ManifoldContext requires the temporal difference stream")
        if len(difference) != len(normalized):
            raise ValueError("the difference stream must match the number of scales")
        return [channel_norm(self.norm_diff, d) for d in difference]

    def scale_temperature(self, query_scale):
        base = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, self.heads, 1, 1)
        return base * self.scale_log_temperature[query_scale].exp()

    def support_term(self, sources, support):
        pooled = []
        for x, grid in zip(sources, self.grid_sizes):
            cell = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(self.support_head(x).sigmoid(), grid)
            pooled.append(cell.flatten(2).transpose(1, 2).reshape(cell.shape[0], -1))
        return torch.cat(pooled, dim=1).clamp_min(1e-6)

    def aggregate(self, probability, values):
        """Klein barycenter of the lifted values, mapped back to tangent space."""
        centroid = probability @ lift(values)
        norm2 = centroid[..., :1].square() - centroid[..., 1:].square().sum(-1, keepdim=True)
        return centroid[..., 1:] / norm2.clamp_min(1).sqrt()

    @staticmethod
    def position_channels(height, width, reference, scale_index):
        ys = torch.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, height, device=reference.device, dtype=reference.dtype)
        xs = torch.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, width, device=reference.device, dtype=reference.dtype)
        grid_y = ys.view(1, 1, height, 1).expand(reference.shape[0], 1, height, width)
        grid_x = xs.view(1, 1, 1, width).expand(reference.shape[0], 1, height, width)
        scale = torch.full_like(grid_x, (scale_index + 1) / 4.0)
        return torch.cat((grid_x, grid_y, scale), dim=1)

    def readout(self, feature, probability, values, occupancy, prototype_occupancy,
                scale_index=None):
        height, width = feature.shape[-2:]
        retrieved = merge_heads(self.aggregate(probability, values), height, width)
        own_value = self.v(feature)
        discrepancy = retrieved - retrieved.mean((-2, -1), keepdim=True)
        retrieved_occupancy = (probability @ prototype_occupancy[:, None]).mean(1)
        retrieved_occupancy = retrieved_occupancy.transpose(1, 2).reshape(-1, 1, height, width)
        index = 0 if scale_index is None else scale_index
        position = self.position_channels(height, width, feature, index)
        gate = self.gate(torch.cat((own_value, discrepancy, occupancy,
                                    retrieved_occupancy, position), 1)).sigmoid()
        gate_map = gate[:, :, None].expand(-1, -1, self.head_dim, -1, -1).flatten(1, 2)
        gated = gate_map * discrepancy
        level = self.level(retrieved.mean((-2, -1)))
        update = self.out(gated) + self.level_scale.tanh() * level[:, :, None, None]
        return update, gate, retrieved, own_value, discrepancy, gated

================================================================================
# FILE: models/model\layers\pyramid_context.py
================================================================================
"""Selective change prototypes and self-referenced pyramid retrieval."""
import math

import torch
from torch import nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


def channel_norm(layer, x):
    return layer(x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1)).permute(0, 3, 1, 2)


def regions(x, grid):
    """B,heads,height,width,D -> B,heads,regions,pixels,D."""
    batch, heads, height, width, dim = x.shape
    return x.reshape(batch, heads, grid, height // grid, grid, width // grid, dim).permute(
        0, 1, 2, 4, 3, 5, 6).reshape(batch, heads, grid * grid, -1, dim)


def feature_regions(x, grid):
    return regions(x.permute(0, 2, 3, 1).unsqueeze(1), grid).squeeze(1)


def split_heads(x, heads):
    batch, channels, height, width = x.shape
    return x.reshape(batch, heads, channels // heads, height, width).permute(0, 1, 3, 4, 2)


def merge_heads(x, height, width):
    return x.transpose(-1, -2).reshape(x.shape[0], -1, height, width)


def project_tokens(layer, tokens, heads):
    projected = F.linear(tokens, layer.weight[:, :, 0, 0])
    return projected.reshape(projected.shape[0], -1, heads, projected.shape[-1] // heads).transpose(1, 2)


def assignments(probability, grid):
    """Sharpen class membership within each region and measure class support."""
    positive = feature_regions(probability, grid).squeeze(-1)
    memberships = torch.stack((positive, 1 - positive), dim=2)
    weights = (memberships + 1e-6).square()
    weights = weights / weights.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    confidence = (weights * memberships.square()).sum(-1).clamp_min(1e-6)
    return weights, confidence.flatten(1, 2)


def pool_features(feature, weights, grid):
    return torch.einsum("brtp,brpc->brtc", weights, feature_regions(feature, grid)).flatten(1, 2)


def source_bias(bias, query_scale, counts):
    return torch.cat([(bias[:, query_scale, scale] - math.log(count))[:, None].expand(-1, count)
                      for scale, count in enumerate(counts)], dim=-1)[None, :, None]


def highpass(x):
    return x - F.avg_pool2d(F.pad(x, (1, 1, 1, 1), mode="replicate"), 3, 1)


def rms(x):
    return x.square().mean().sqrt()


def attention_diagnostics(probability, counts):
    mass = probability.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
    conditional = probability / mass.clamp_min(1e-8)
    tv = (conditional - conditional.mean(-2, keepdim=True)).abs().sum(-1) / 2
    return dict(
        entropy=float(-(conditional * conditional.clamp_min(1e-9).log()).sum(-1).mean()
                      / math.log(probability.shape[-1])),
        scale_mass=torch.stack([p.sum(-1) for p in conditional.split(counts, -1)], -1).mean((0, 2)).tolist(),
        context_mass_mean=float(mass.mean()), context_mass_min=float(mass.min()),
        context_mass_max=float(mass.max()), context_mass_std=float(mass.std(unbiased=False)),
        self_mass_mean=float((1-mass).mean()),
        query_attention_tv=float(tv.mean()), query_attention_tv_per_head=tv.mean((0, 2)).tolist())


class ResidualPyramidContext(nn.Module):
    """Shared compression/readout; subclasses define Euclidean or Lorentz keys."""
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    compression_power = 2
    support_power = 2
    self_reference = True

    def __init__(self, channels=64, heads=4, grid_sizes=(8, 4, 4, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        self.heads = heads
        self.grid_sizes = tuple(grid_sizes)
        self.tokens_per_scale = tuple(2 * grid ** 2 for grid in grid_sizes)
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.qk = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.v = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.local_dw = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 3, padding=1, padding_mode="replicate",
                                  groups=channels, bias=False)
        self.local_out = nn.Conv2d(channels, channels, 1, bias=False)
        self.scale_bias = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(heads, 4, 4))
        self.raw_temperature = nn.Parameter(torch.full((heads,), math.log(math.expm1(.5))))
        self.gamma_context = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(4, channels))
        self.gamma_detail = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(2, channels))
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def attention(self, query, key, confidence, query_scale):
        temperature = F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).view(1, self.heads, 1, 1)
        logits = -self.squared_distance(query, key) / (2 * temperature.square())
        logits = logits + confidence.log()[:, None, None] + source_bias(
            self.scale_bias, query_scale, self.tokens_per_scale)
        # The query's own key has distance zero and unit support. Its value is
        # retained locally; a shared softmax makes context adoption query-specific.
        return torch.cat((logits, torch.zeros_like(logits[..., :1])), -1).softmax(-1)[..., :-1]

    def forward(self, tar):
        normalized = [channel_norm(self.norm, x) for x in tar]
        guide = [self.guide(x) for x in normalized]
        compression = [assignments(g.sigmoid(), grid) for g, grid in zip(guide, self.grid_sizes)]
        prototypes = [pool_features(x, w, grid) for x, (w, _), grid in
                      zip(normalized, compression, self.grid_sizes)]
        query = [self.project_queries(x) for x in normalized]
        keys = [self.pool_keys(q, p, w, grid) for q, p, (w, _), grid in
                zip(query, prototypes, compression, self.grid_sizes)]
        key = torch.cat(keys, dim=2)
        confidence = torch.cat([r for _, r in compression], dim=1)
        values = project_tokens(self.v, torch.cat(prototypes, dim=1), self.heads)
        enhanced, diagnostics = [], {}
        for index, (original, x, q) in enumerate(zip(tar, normalized, query)):
            height, width = x.shape[-2:]
            probability = self.attention(q.flatten(2, 3), key, confidence, index)
            retrieved = merge_heads(probability @ values, height, width)
            own_value = self.v(x)
            mass = probability.sum(-1, keepdim=True)
            mass_map = merge_heads(mass.expand(-1, -1, -1, x.shape[1] // self.heads), height, width)
            difference = retrieved - mass_map * own_value
            update = self.out(difference)
            context_residual = self.gamma_context[index][None, :, None, None] * update
            result = original + context_residual
            local = None
            local_residual = torch.zeros_like(original)
            if index < 2:
                local = self.local_out(self.local_dw(highpass(x)))
                local_residual = self.gamma_detail[index][None, :, None, None] * local
                result = result + local_residual
            enhanced.append(result)
            if self.collect_diag:
                with torch.no_grad():
                    base = rms(original).clamp_min(1e-8)
                    weights, reliability = compression[index]
                    diag = dict(
                        queries=height * width, contexts=key.shape[2],
                        gamma_context=float(self.gamma_context[index].mean()),
                        gamma_context_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_context[index])),
                        gamma_detail=float(self.gamma_detail[index].mean()) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        gamma_detail_rms=float(rms(self.gamma_detail[index])) if index < 2 else 0.,
                        context_rms=float(rms(update)), branch_rms=float(rms(update)),
                        local_rms=float(rms(local)) if local is not None else 0.,
                        feature_rms=float(base), relative_update_rms=float(rms(difference)),
                        residual_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual)),
                        residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual + local_residual) / base),
                        context_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(context_residual) / base),
                        local_residual_to_feature_rms=float(rms(local_residual) / base),
                        guide_mean=float(guide[index].sigmoid().mean()),
                        reliability_mean=float(reliability.mean()), reliability_min=float(reliability.min()),
                        retrieved_rms=float(rms(retrieved)), self_value_rms=float(rms(own_value)),
                        self_subtraction_rms=float(rms(mass_map * own_value)),
                        retrieved_spatial_rms=float(rms(retrieved-retrieved.mean((-2,-1),keepdim=True))),
                        retrieved_self_cosine=float(F.cosine_similarity(retrieved.flatten(1),own_value.flatten(1)).mean()),
                        assignment_tv=float((weights[:, :, 0] - weights[:, :, 1]).abs().sum(-1).mean() / 2),
                        qk_weight_rms=float(rms(self.qk.weight)), v_weight_rms=float(rms(self.v.weight)),
                        out_weight_rms=float(rms(self.out.weight)),
                        temperature=F.softplus(self.raw_temperature).tolist(),
                        finite=bool(torch.isfinite(result).all()))
                    diag.update(attention_diagnostics(probability, self.tokens_per_scale))
                    diag.update(self.geometry_diagnostics(q, keys[index]))
                    diagnostics[f"stage{index + 1}"] = diag
        if self.collect_diag:
            self.last_diag = diagnostics
        return enhanced, dict(guide_logits=tuple(guide))


class AuxiliaryContext(nn.Module):
    """CTRL retains the same change-guide supervision on unmodified TAR features."""
    geometry = "none"
    value_geometry = "euclidean"
    grid_sizes = (8, 4, 4, 4)
    tokens_per_scale = (0, 0, 0, 0)
    compression_power = 0
    support_power = 0
    self_reference = False

    def __init__(self, channels=64):
        super().__init__()
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(channels)
        self.guide = nn.Conv2d(channels, 1, 1)
        self.collect_diag = False
        self.last_diag = {}

    def forward(self, tar):
        return list(tar), dict(guide_logits=tuple(self.guide(channel_norm(self.norm, x)) for x in tar))
